1	<h1 align="center">FlashPrefill</h1>
2	
3	This repository provides the code for the paper [FlashPrefill: Instantaneous Pattern Discovery and Thresholding for Ultra-Fast Long-Context Prefilling](http://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06199)
4	
5	## Abstract
6	Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. While various sparse attention mechanisms have been explored, they typically suffer from either significant search latency or insufficient sparsity. In this paper, we propose FlashPrefill, a framework enabling ultra-fast prefilling via instantaneous pattern discovery and thresholding. FlashPrefill leverages a fast block-searching technique to simultaneously locate dynamic vertical, slash, and block-sparse attention patterns. Crucially, it introduces a dynamic thresholding mechanism that bypasses the prohibitive overhead of sorting or accumulating attention scores while effectively eliminating the long-tail distribution to enhance sparsity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FlashPrefill achieves a substantial leap in efficiency, delivering an unprecedented $27.78\times$ speedup on 256K sequences. Notably, unlike existing methods that incur efficiency degradation on shorter contexts, FlashPrefill maintains a $1.71\times$ speedup even at a 4K context length, demonstrating its robustness and practical utility across varying sequence scales.
7	
8	## Requirements
9	To use FlashPrefill, you will need the following packages:
10	- `torch==2.9.0`
11	- `triton==3.3.0`
12	- `transformers==4.56.1`
13	- `flash_attn==2.8.3` (optional)
14	- `vllm==0.10.0 or 0.12.0` (optional)
15	
16	## Quick Start
17	For evaluation instructions, please refer to the README file in each respective subfolder. Follow the previous methods, efficiency is tested with the native forward (batch_size==1).
18	
19	## Integration with vLLM
20	
21	We have integrated FlashPrefill into vLLM (version 0.10.0 or 0.12.0). You can use the following code to directly patch FlashPrefill into vLLM.
22	
23	```python
24	from patches import patch_loader_vllm_0_10_0
25	# from patches import patch_loader_vllm_0_12_0
26	from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
27	
28	model = LLM(
29	    model=model_name,
30	    tokenizer=model_name,
31	    trust_remote_code=True,
32	    dtype="bfloat16",
33	    tensor_parallel_size=4,
34	    max_model_len=32 * 1024, 
35	    gpu_memory_utilization=0.9,
36	    enable_chunked_prefill=False,
37	    enable_prefix_caching=False
38	)
39	sampling_params = SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=64)
40	
41	model.generate(prompts=[prompt], sampling_params=sampling_params)
42	output = outputs[0].outputs[0].text
43	```
44	
45	## Cite
46	```latex
47	@article{fan2026flashprefill,
48	      title={FlashPrefill: Instantaneous Pattern Discovery and Thresholding for Ultra-Fast Long-Context Prefilling}, 
49	      author={Qihang Fan and Huaibo Huang and Zhiying Wu and Juqiu Wang and Bingning Wang and Ran He},
50	      year={2026},
51	      journal={arXiv preprint arXiv:2603.06199},
52	      eprint={2603.06199},
53	      archivePrefix={arXiv},
54	      primaryClass={cs.CL},
55	      url={https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06199}, 
56	}
57	```
58	
59	## Acknowledgments
60	The codebase is based on the [MInference](https://github.com/microsoft/MInference), [XAttention](https://github.com/mit-han-lab/x-attention), and [FlexPrefill](https://github.com/ByteDance-Seed/FlexPrefill), we acknowledge the contributions of these works.
61

```
1	from numpy import dtype
2	import torch
3	import triton
4	import triton.language as tl
5	from fla.utils import autocast_custom_fwd, contiguous
6	import math

8	def get_cu_tile_seqlens(cu_seqlens, BLOCK_SIZE):
9	    seqlens = cu_seqlens[1:] - cu_seqlens[:-1]
10	    tiles_per_seq = (seqlens + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) // BLOCK_SIZE
11	    tiles_2_per_seq = tiles_per_seq ** 2
12	    cu_tile_seqlens = torch.zeros(cu_seqlens.shape, dtype=torch.int32, device=cu_seqlens.device)
13	    cu_tile_seqlens[1:] = torch.cumsum(tiles_per_seq, dim=0)
14	
15	    cu_tile_2_seqlens = torch.zeros(cu_seqlens.shape, dtype=torch.int32, device=cu_seqlens.device)
16	    cu_tile_2_seqlens[1:] = torch.cumsum(tiles_2_per_seq, dim=0)
17	    
18	    return cu_tile_seqlens, cu_tile_2_seqlens
19	
20	def get_mean_configs():
21	    configs = []
22	    warps = [4, 8]
23	    stages = [2, 3, 4, 5]
24	    for w in warps:
25	        for s in stages:
26	            configs.append(
27	                triton.Config({}, num_warps=w, num_stages=s)
28	            )
29	    return configs
30	@triton.autotune(
31	    configs=get_mean_configs(),
32	    key=['max_seq_len', 'num_q_heads'],
33	)
34	@triton.jit
35	def compute_mean_vector_varlen(
36	    Q_ptr, mQ_ptr,
37	    cu_seqlens_ptr,      
38	    cu_tile_seqlens_ptr, 
39	    stride_qs, stride_qh, stride_qd,   
40	    stride_mqt, stride_mqh, stride_mqd, 
41	    num_q_heads,
42	    max_seq_len,         
43	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
44	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr
45	):
46	
47	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64) 
48	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)        
49	
50	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
51	    offset_q_head = offset_zh % num_q_heads
52	
53	    start_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
54	    end_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
55	    curr_seq_len = end_token_idx - start_token_idx
56	
57	    if query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE >= curr_seq_len:
58	        return
59	
60	    start_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
61	    end_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
62	    curr_tile_len = end_tile_idx - start_tile_idx
63	
64	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_qs + offset_q_head * stride_qh
65	    mQ_base_ptr = mQ_ptr + start_tile_idx * stride_mqt + offset_q_head * stride_mqh
66	
67	    offset_q = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE)
68	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
69	
70	    load_mask = (offset_q[:, None] < curr_seq_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD)
71	    q = tl.load(
72	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qs + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
73	        mask=load_mask,
74	        other=0.0
75	    )
76	
77	    q_sum = tl.sum(q, axis=0)
78	    
79	    q_mask = (offset_q < curr_seq_len)
80	    num_ele = tl.sum(q_mask.to(tl.int32)).to(q.dtype)
81	    
82	    q_mean = tl.where(num_ele > 0, q_sum / num_ele, 0.0)
83	
84	    store_ptr = mQ_base_ptr + query_tile_index * stride_mqt + offset_dim * stride_mqd
85	    tl.store(store_ptr, q_mean, mask=(offset_dim < D_HEAD) & (query_tile_index < curr_tile_len))
86	
87

90	    k_tiles = [32, 64, 128]
91	    warps = [4, 8]
92	    stages = [2, 3, 4, 5]
93	
94	    for k in k_tiles:

106	@triton.jit
107	def compute_block_score_varlen(
108	    Q_ptr, K_ptr, scale,
109	    sc_ptr, mx_ptr,
110	    cu_seqlens_ptr,
111	    cu_tile_seqlens_ptr,
112	    cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr,
113	    stride_qm, stride_qh, stride_qd,
114	    stride_kn, stride_kh, stride_kd,
115	    stride_scl, stride_sch,
116	    stride_mxl, stride_mxh,
117	    num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
118	    query_len_max, sub_key_len_max,
119	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
120	    K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
121	    K_STRIDE: tl.constexpr,
122	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr
123	):
124	    '''
125	    optimized with stride
126	    '''
127	    num_stride_per_block: tl.constexpr = BLOCK_SIZE // K_STRIDE
128	    num_block_per_tile: tl.constexpr = K_TILE_SIZE // num_stride_per_block
129	
130	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
131	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
132	
133	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
134	    group_size = num_q_heads // num_k_heads
135	
136	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
137	    offset_k_heads = offset_q_heads // group_size
138	
139	    start_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
140	    end_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
141	    curr_seq_len = end_token_idx - start_token_idx
142	
143	    if query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE >= curr_seq_len:
144	        return
145	    
146	    start_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
147	    end_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
148	    curr_tiles_len = end_tile_idx - start_tile_idx
149	
150	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_qm + offset_q_heads * stride_qh
151	    K_base_ptr = K_ptr + start_tile_idx * stride_kn + offset_k_heads * stride_kh
152	
153	    start_score_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
154	
155	    sc_base_ptr = sc_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_scl + offset_q_heads * stride_sch
156	    mx_base_ptr = mx_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_mxl + offset_q_heads * stride_mxh
157	
158	    offset_q = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE)
159	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
160	
161	    q_index_max = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + BLOCK_SIZE - 1
162	    q_index = offset_q
163	
164	    q = tl.load(
165	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qm + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
166	        mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_seq_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD),
167	        other=0.0
168	    )
169	
170	    lo = 0
171	    hi = tl.cdiv(q_index_max + 1, K_STRIDE)
172	
173	    sm_scale = scale * 1.4426950408889634
174	
175	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE):
176	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
177	        k_index_min = offset_k * K_STRIDE
178	        k_index_max = offset_k * K_STRIDE + K_STRIDE - 1
179	        k = tl.load(
180	            K_base_ptr + offset_k[:, None] * stride_kn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_kd,
181	            mask=(offset_k[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD),
182	            other=0.0
183	        )
184	
185	        qk = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k))
186	        causal_mask = (q_index[:, None] >= k_index_max[None, :]) # (block_size, k_tile_size)
187	
188	        qk = tl.where(causal_mask, qk, float('-inf'))
189	        qk *= sm_scale # (block_size, (num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
190	
191	        qk = tl.reshape(qk, (BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
192	        m_i_block = tl.max(qk, axis=2) #(block_size, num_block_per_tile)
193	        m_i_block = tl.max(m_i_block, axis=0) # (num_block_per_tile)
194	
195	        qk_block = qk - m_i_block[None, :, None]
196	        p_block = tl.exp2(qk_block)
197	
198	        causal_mask_block = tl.reshape(causal_mask, (BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
199	        p_block = tl.where(causal_mask_block, p_block, 0.0) #(BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block)
200	
201	
202	        p_block = tl.sum(p_block, 2) 
203	        p_block = tl.sum(p_block, 0) # num_block_per_tile
204	
205	        offset_k_blocks = (j // K_TILE_SIZE) * num_block_per_tile + tl.arange(0, num_block_per_tile)
206	
207	        tl.store(
208	            sc_base_ptr + (query_tile_index * curr_tiles_len + offset_k_blocks) * stride_scl,
209	            p_block,
210	            mask=(query_tile_index < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k_blocks < curr_tiles_len)
211	        )
212	
213	        tl.store(
214	            mx_base_ptr + (query_tile_index * curr_tiles_len + offset_k_blocks) * stride_mxl,
215	            m_i_block,
216	            mask=(query_tile_index < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k_blocks < curr_tiles_len)
217	        )
218	
219	def get_score_sub_max_configs():
220	    configs = []
221	    q_tiles = [32, 64, 128]
222	    k_tiles = [32, 64, 128]
223	    warps = [4, 8]
224	    stages = [2, 3, 4, 5]
225	
226	    for q in q_tiles:
227	        for k in k_tiles:
228	            for w in warps:
229	                for s in stages:
230	                    # 过滤掉一些可能不合理的组合

235	@triton.autotune(
236	    configs=get_score_sub_max_configs(),

238	)
239	@triton.jit
240	def compute_block_sub_max_varlen(
241	    mx_ptr, out_ptr,
242	    cu_tile_seqlens_ptr,
243	    cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr,
244	    stride_mxl, stride_mxh,
245	    stride_outl, stride_outh,
246	    num_q_heads,
247	    block_len_max,
248	    Q_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
249	    K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr
250	):
251	    '''
252	    get_max varlen
253	    '''
254	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
255	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
256	
257	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
258	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
259	
260	    start_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
261	    end_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
262	    curr_tiles_len = end_tile_idx - start_tile_idx
263	
264	    if query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE >= curr_tiles_len:
265	        return
266	
267	    start_score_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
268	
269	    mx_base_ptr = mx_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_mxl + offset_q_heads * stride_mxh
270	    out_base_ptr = out_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_outl + offset_q_heads * stride_outh
271	
272	    offset_q = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
273	
274	    lo = 0
275	    hi = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + Q_TILE_SIZE
276	
277	    m_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), float('-inf'), dtype=tl.float32)
278	
279	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE): #type: ignore
280	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
281	        mx_tile = tl.load(
282	            mx_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_mxl,
283	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len),
284	            other=float('-inf')
285	        )
286	        m_i = tl.maximum(m_i, tl.max(mx_tile, axis=1))
287	
288	    # m_i = tl.where(m_i > float('-inf'), m_i, float('inf'))
289	
290	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE): #type: ignore
291	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
292	        mx_tile = tl.load(
293	            mx_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_mxl,
294	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len),
295	            other=0.0
296	        )
297	
298	        out_tile = tl.exp2(mx_tile - m_i[:, None])
299	
300	        tl.store(
301	            out_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_outl,
302	            out_tile,
303	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len)
304	        )
305	        
306

336	    window_size,
337	    last_n_block,
338	    num_q_heads,
339	    block_len_max,
340	    total_seq_len, # this is the number of block
341	    Q_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
342	    K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
343	):
344	    '''
345	    filter blocks
346	    '''
347	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
348	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
349	
350	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
351	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
352	
353	    start_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
354	    end_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
355	    curr_tiles_len = end_tile_idx - start_tile_idx
356	
357	    if query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE >= curr_tiles_len:
358	        return
359	    
360	    start_score_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
361	
362	    sc_base_ptr = sc_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_scl + offset_q_heads * stride_sch
363	    out_base_ptr = out_ptr + start_score_tile_idx * stride_outl + offset_q_heads * stride_outh
364	    out_number_base_ptr = out_number_ptr + (total_seq_len * offset_q_heads + start_tile_idx) * stride_numberl
365	
366	    offset_q = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
367	    mask_last_full = (offset_q >= (curr_tiles_len - last_n_block))[:, None]
368	
369	    lo = 0
370	    hi = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + Q_TILE_SIZE
371	    # hi = tl.minimum(curr_tiles_len, query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + Q_TILE_SIZE)
372	
373	    m_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), float('-inf'), dtype=tl.float32)
374	    number_i = tl.zeros((Q_TILE_SIZE,), dtype=tl.int32)
375	
376	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE): #type: ignore
377	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
378	        sc_tile = tl.load(
379	            sc_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_scl,
380	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len),
381	            other=float('-inf')
382	        )
383	        m_i = tl.maximum(m_i, tl.max(sc_tile, axis=1))
384	
385	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE): #type: ignore
386	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
387	        sc_tile = tl.load(
388	            sc_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_scl,
389	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len),
390	            other=float('-inf')
391	        )
392	        out_tile_mask = (sc_tile >= alpha * m_i[:, None])
393	        window_mask = (offset_q[:, None] >= offset_k[None, :]) & ((offset_q[:, None] - offset_k[None, :]) < window_size)
394	        sink_mask = (offset_k[None, :] < attention_sink)
395	        causal_mask = (offset_q[:, None] >= offset_k[None, :])
396	        out_tile_mask = (out_tile_mask | window_mask | sink_mask | mask_last_full) & causal_mask
397	        out_tile = tl.where(out_tile_mask, offset_k[None, :], -1)
398	        tl.store(
399	            out_base_ptr + (offset_q[:, None] * curr_tiles_len + offset_k[None, :]) * stride_outl,
400	            out_tile,
401	            mask=(offset_q[:, None] < curr_tiles_len) & (offset_k[None, :] < curr_tiles_len)
402	        )
403	        number_i += tl.sum(out_tile_mask.to(tl.int32), axis=1)
404	
405	    tl.store(
406	        out_number_base_ptr + offset_q * stride_numberl,
407	        number_i,
408	        mask=offset_q < curr_tiles_len
409	    )
410	
411	
412	def get_configs():
413	    configs = []
414	    tile_sizes = [
415	        (64, 64),   
416	        (128, 64),  
417	        (64, 128)   
418	    ]
419	    warps_list = [4, 8]
420	    stages_list = [2, 3, 4, 5]
421	
422	    for (q_tile, k_tile) in tile_sizes:
423	        for w in warps_list:
424	            for s in stages_list:
425	                if q_tile == 128 and w == 4:
426	                    continue
427	                
428	                configs.append(
429	                    triton.Config(
430	                        {'Q_TILE_SIZE': q_tile, 'K_TILE_SIZE': k_tile}, 
431	                        num_warps=w, 
432	                        num_stages=s
433	                    )
434	                )
435	    return configs
436	
437	@triton.autotune(
438	    configs=get_configs(),
439	    key=['query_len', 'key_len', 'num_q_heads', 'num_k_heads', 'BLOCK_SIZE'],
440	    prune_configs_by={
441	        'early_config_prune': lambda configs, named_args, **kwargs: [
442	            c for c in configs 
443	            if c.kwargs['Q_TILE_SIZE'] <= kwargs['BLOCK_SIZE'] 
444	            and c.kwargs['K_TILE_SIZE'] <= kwargs['BLOCK_SIZE']
445	        ]
446	    }
447	)
448	@triton.jit
449	def _flash_block_sparse_varlen(
450	    Q_ptr, K_ptr, V_ptr, O_ptr, index_ptr, valid_ptr, scale,
451	    stride_qm, stride_qh, stride_qd,
452	    stride_kn, stride_kh, stride_kd,
453	    stride_vn, stride_vh, stride_vd,
454	    stride_om, stride_oh, stride_od,
455	    stride_indexl,
456	    stride_validl,
457	    cu_seqlens_ptr,
458	    cu_tile_seqlens_ptr,
459	    cu_tile_2_seqlens_ptr,
460	    total_seq_len, 
461	    num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
462	    Q_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr, K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
463	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
464	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr
465	):
466	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
467	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
468	
469	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
470	    group_size = num_q_heads // num_k_heads
471	    index_group_size = BLOCK_SIZE // Q_TILE_SIZE
472	    '''
473	    BLOCK_SIZE >= Q_TILE_SIZE
474	    BLOCK_SIZE >= K_TILE_SIZE
475	    only support key_len == query_len
476	    '''
477	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
478	    offset_k_heads = offset_q_heads // group_size
479	
480	    start_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
481	    end_token_idx = tl.load(cu_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch + 1)
482	    curr_seq_len = end_token_idx - start_token_idx
483	
484	    if query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE >= curr_seq_len:
485	        return
486	    
487	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_qm + offset_q_heads * stride_qh
488	    K_base_ptr = K_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_kn + offset_k_heads * stride_kh
489	    V_base_ptr = V_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_vn + offset_k_heads * stride_vh
490	    O_base_ptr = O_ptr + start_token_idx * stride_om + offset_q_heads * stride_oh
491	    
492	    start_tile_idx = tl.load(cu_tile_seqlens_ptr + offset_batch)
493	
494	    valid_base_ptr = valid_ptr + (total_seq_len * offset_q_heads + start_tile_idx) * stride_validl
495	
496	    valid_start = tl.load(valid_base_ptr + (query_tile_index // index_group_size) * stride_validl)
497	    valid_end = tl.load(valid_base_ptr + ((query_tile_index // index_group_size) + 1) * stride_validl)
498	
499	    index_base_ptr = index_ptr + valid_start * stride_indexl
500	
501	    offset_q = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
502	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
503	
504	    q_mask = (offset_q[:, None] < curr_seq_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD)
505	    q = tl.load(
506	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qm + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
507	        mask=q_mask,
508	        other=0.0
509	    )
510	
511	    sm_scale = scale * 1.4426950408889634
512	
513	    m_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), float("-inf"), dtype=tl.float32)
514	    l_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), 1.0, dtype=tl.float32)
515	    acc = tl.zeros((Q_TILE_SIZE, D_HEAD), dtype=tl.float32)
516	
517	    lo = 0
518	    hi = valid_end - valid_start
519	
520	    block_index = tl.load(index_base_ptr + lo * stride_indexl, mask=(lo < hi), other=0)
521	
522	    for i in range(lo, hi):
523	        next_block_index = tl.load(index_base_ptr + (i+1) * stride_indexl, mask=(i+1 < hi), other=0)
524	        key_start_index = block_index * BLOCK_SIZE
525	        key_end_index = (block_index + 1) * BLOCK_SIZE
526	
527	        is_diagonal_block = (block_index == (query_tile_index // index_group_size))
528	
529	        for j in range(key_start_index, key_end_index, K_TILE_SIZE):
530	            k_index = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
531	            k_j = tl.load(
532	                K_base_ptr + k_index[:, None] * stride_kn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_kd,
533	                mask=(k_index[:, None]<curr_seq_len) & (offset_dim[None, :]<D_HEAD),
534	                other=0.0
535	            )
536	            v_j = tl.load(
537	                V_base_ptr + k_index[:, None] * stride_vn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_vd,
538	                mask=(k_index[:, None]<curr_seq_len) & (offset_dim[None, :]<D_HEAD),
539	                other=0.0
540	            )
541	
542	            qk = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k_j))
543	            if is_diagonal_block:
544	                dist = offset_q[:, None] - k_index[None, :]
545	                causal_mask = (dist >= 0)
546	                qk = tl.where(causal_mask, qk, float('-inf'))
547	            qk *= sm_scale
548	            m_i_new = tl.maximum(m_i, tl.max(qk, axis=1))
549	            qk -= m_i_new[:, None]
550	            p = tl.exp2(qk) 
551	            # if is_diagonal_block:
552	            #     p = tl.where(causal_mask, p, 0.0)
553	            lij = tl.sum(p, 1)
554	            alpha = tl.exp2(m_i - m_i_new)
555	            alpha_mask = (alpha != alpha)
556	            alpha = tl.where(alpha_mask, 1.0, alpha)
557	            acc *= alpha[:, None]
558	            l_i = l_i * alpha + lij
559	            p = p.to(v_j.dtype) 
560	            acc += tl.dot(p, v_j)
561	            m_i = m_i_new
562	        block_index = next_block_index
563	
564	    l_rec = 1 / l_i[:, None]
565	    acc = acc * l_rec
566	
567	    o_offset_m = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
568	    o_offset_d = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
569	
570	    o_mask = (o_offset_m[:, None] < curr_seq_len) & (o_offset_d[None, :] < D_HEAD)
571	    tl.store(
572	        O_base_ptr + o_offset_m[:, None] * stride_om + o_offset_d[None, :] * stride_od,
573	        acc.to(q.dtype),
574	        mask=o_mask
575	    )
576	
577	class FlashPrefill_varlen(torch.autograd.Function):
578	
579	    @staticmethod
580	    @contiguous
581	    @autocast_custom_fwd
582	    def forward(ctx, q, k, v, o, cu_seqlens, max_seq_len, block_size=128, k_stride=128, attention_sink=2, window=4, alpha=0.7, last_n_blocks=2):
583	        '''
584	        q: (l n d)
585	        k: (l n d)
586	        v: (l n d)
587	        o: (l n d)
588	        cu_seqlens: (batch + 1,)
589	        '''
590	        assert block_size == k_stride, "block_size must equal to k_stride"
591	        cu_tile_seqlens, cu_tile_2_seqlens = get_cu_tile_seqlens(cu_seqlens, block_size)
592	        _, num_q_heads, head_dim = q.shape
593	        _, num_k_heads, _ = k.shape
594	        batch_size = cu_seqlens.shape[0] - 1
595	
596	        mean_k = torch.empty(cu_tile_seqlens[-1], num_k_heads, head_dim, dtype=k.dtype, device=k.device) #type: ignore
597	
598	        def grid_mean_varlen(meta):
599	            return (triton.cdiv(max_seq_len, k_stride), batch_size * num_k_heads, 1)
600	        compute_mean_vector_varlen[grid_mean_varlen](
601	            k, mean_k,
602	            cu_seqlens,
603	            cu_tile_seqlens,
604	            *k.stride(),
605	            *mean_k.stride(),
606	            num_k_heads,
607	            max_seq_len,
608	            block_size,
609	            head_dim
610	        )
611	
612	        query_len_max = max_seq_len
613	        block_len_max = sub_key_len_max = triton.cdiv(max_seq_len, k_stride)
614	
615	        output_score = torch.full(size=(int(cu_tile_2_seqlens[-1]), num_q_heads), fill_value=float('-inf'), dtype=torch.float32, device=q.device)
616	        output_max = torch.full(size=(int(cu_tile_2_seqlens[-1]), num_q_heads), fill_value=float('-inf'), dtype=torch.float32, device=q.device)
617	
618	
619	        def grid_block_score_varlen(meta):
620	            return (triton.cdiv(max_seq_len, block_size), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
621	        compute_block_score_varlen[grid_block_score_varlen](
622	            q, mean_k, 1 / (head_dim ** 0.5), 
623	            output_score, output_max,
624	            cu_seqlens,
625	            cu_tile_seqlens,
626	            cu_tile_2_seqlens,
627	            *q.stride(),
628	            *mean_k.stride(),
629	            *output_score.stride(),
630	            *output_max.stride(),
631	            num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
632	            query_len_max, sub_key_len_max,
633	            block_size,
634	            K_STRIDE=block_size,
635	            D_HEAD=head_dim
636	        )
637	
638	        output_max_sub_max = torch.full_like(output_max, fill_value=0)
639	
640	        def grid_block_sub_max_varlen(meta):
641	            return (triton.cdiv(block_len_max, meta["Q_TILE_SIZE"]), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
642	        compute_block_sub_max_varlen[grid_block_sub_max_varlen](
643	            output_max, output_max_sub_max,
644	            cu_tile_seqlens,
645	            cu_tile_2_seqlens,
646	            *output_max.stride(),
647	            *output_max_sub_max.stride(),
648	            num_q_heads,
649	            block_len_max
650	        )
651	
652	        # print(torch.any(torch.isinf(output_max_sub_max)))
653	
654	        # print(output_score)
655	
656	        output_score = output_score * output_max_sub_max
657	
658	        # print(output_score)
659	
660	        total_block_seq_len = cu_tile_seqlens[-1]
661	
662	        out_number = torch.full(size=(total_block_seq_len * num_q_heads,), fill_value=0, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
663	        out_index = torch.full(size=(int(cu_tile_2_seqlens[-1]), num_q_heads), fill_value=-1, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
664	
665	        def grid_compute_block_max_varlen(meta):
666	            return (triton.cdiv(block_len_max, meta["Q_TILE_SIZE"]), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
667	        compute_block_max_varlen[grid_compute_block_max_varlen](
668	            output_score, out_index, out_number,
669	            cu_tile_seqlens,
670	            cu_tile_2_seqlens,
671	            *output_score.stride(),
672	            *out_index.stride(),
673	            *out_number.stride(),
674	            alpha,
675	            attention_sink,
676	            window,
677	            last_n_blocks,
678	            num_q_heads,
679	            block_len_max,
680	            total_block_seq_len.item()
681	        )
682	
683	        out_index = out_index.transpose(0, 1).flatten(0, 1)
684	
685	        # index_for_debug = out_index.reshape(num_q_heads, -1)
686	        # seq_len = int(math.sqrt(int(index_for_debug.shape[-1])))
687	        # print('*'*120)
688	        # print(seq_len)
689	        # index_for_debug = index_for_debug.reshape(num_q_heads, seq_len, seq_len)
690	        # print(index_for_debug[4][-4])
691	
692	        # number_for_debug = out_number.reshape(num_q_heads, int(total_block_seq_len)).contiguous()
693	        # print(total_block_seq_len)
694	        # print(number_for_debug[4][-4])
695	
696	        out_index = out_index[out_index!=-1].contiguous()
697	        out_number_cu = torch.zeros(out_number.shape[0]+1, dtype=torch.int32, device=q.device)
698	        out_number_cu[1:] = torch.cumsum(out_number, dim=0)
699	        # print(out_number_cu[-1])
700	
701	
702	
703	        def grid_flash_block_sparse_varlen(meta):
704	            return (triton.cdiv(max_seq_len, meta['Q_TILE_SIZE']), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
705	        _flash_block_sparse_varlen[grid_flash_block_sparse_varlen](
706	            q, k, v, o, out_index, out_number_cu, 1 / (head_dim ** 0.5),
707	            *q.stride(),
708	            *k.stride(),
709	            *v.stride(),
710	            *o.stride(),
711	            *out_index.stride(),
712	            *out_number_cu.stride(),
713	            cu_seqlens,
714	            cu_tile_seqlens,
715	            cu_tile_2_seqlens,
716	            total_block_seq_len.item(),
717	            num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
718	            BLOCK_SIZE=block_size,
719	            D_HEAD=head_dim
720	        )
721	
722	@torch.no_grad()
723	def flash_prefill_varlen_func(
724	    q: torch.Tensor,
725	    k: torch.Tensor,
726	    v: torch.Tensor,
727	    o: torch.Tensor,
728	    cu_seqlens: torch.Tensor,
729	    max_seq_len: int,
730	    alpha: float,
731	    block_size: int=128,
732	    attention_sink: int=2,
733	    window_size: int=4,
734	    last_n_block: int=2,
735	):
736	    '''
737	    q, k, v: (s h d)
738	    '''
739	    FlashPrefill_varlen.apply(
740	        q, k, v, o, cu_seqlens, max_seq_len,
741	        block_size, block_size, attention_sink,
742	        window_size, alpha, last_n_block
743	    )
744
```

```
9	    warps = [4, 8]
10	    stages = [2, 3, 4, 5]
11	    for w in warps:

17	@triton.autotune(

21	@triton.jit
22	def compute_mean_vector(
23	    Q_ptr, mQ_ptr,
24	    stride_qz, stride_qm, stride_qh, stride_qd,
25	    stride_mqz, stride_mqm, stride_mqh, stride_mqd,
26	    num_q_heads,
27	    query_len,
28	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
29	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr
30	):
31	
32	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
33	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
34	
35	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
36	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
37	
38	    offset_q_ptr = offset_batch * stride_qz + offset_q_heads * stride_qh
39	    offset_mq_ptr = offset_batch * stride_mqz + offset_q_heads * stride_mqh
40	
41	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + offset_q_ptr
42	    mQ_base_ptr = mQ_ptr + offset_mq_ptr
43	
44	    offset_q = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE)
45	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
46	
47	    q = tl.load(
48	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qm + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
49	        mask=(offset_q[:, None] < query_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD),
50	        other=0.0
51	    ) # (block, d)
52	
53	    q_sum = tl.sum(q, axis=0) # (d,)
54	
55	    q_mask = (offset_q < query_len)
56	    num_ele = tl.sum(q_mask.to(tl.int32)).to(q.dtype)
57	
58	    q_mean = tl.where(num_ele > 0, q_sum / num_ele, 0.0)
59	
60	    # q_sq_sum = tl.sum(q * q, axis=0)
61	    # q_var = tl.where(num_ele > 0, (q_sq_sum / num_ele) - (q_mean * q_mean), 0.0)
62	
63	    tl.store(
64	        mQ_base_ptr + query_tile_index * stride_mqm + offset_dim * stride_mqd,
65	        q_mean, # + q_var,
66	        mask=offset_dim < D_HEAD
67	    )
68	
69	def get_score_configs():
70	    configs = []
71	    k_tiles = [64, 128, 256]
72	    warps = [4, 8]
73	    stages = [2, 3, 4, 5]
74	
75	    for k in k_tiles:
76	        for w in warps:
77	            for s in stages:
78	                if k == 256 and w == 4: continue 
79	                configs.append(
80	                    triton.Config({'K_TILE_SIZE': k}, num_warps=w, num_stages=s)
81	                )
82	    return configs
83	@triton.autotune(
84	    configs=get_score_configs(),
85	    key=['query_len', 'sub_key_len', 'num_q_heads', 'num_k_heads'],
86	)
87	@triton.jit
88	def compute_block_score(
89	    Q_ptr, K_ptr, scale,
90	    sc_ptr, mx_ptr,
91	    stride_qz, stride_qm, stride_qh, stride_qd,
92	    stride_kz, stride_kn, stride_kh, stride_kd,
93	    stride_scz, stride_scmb, stride_scnb, stride_sch,
94	    stride_mxz, stride_mxmb, stride_mxnb, stride_mxh,
95	    num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
96	    query_len, sub_key_len,
97	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
98	    K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
99	    K_STRIDE: tl.constexpr,
100	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr
101	):
102	    '''
103	    optimized with stride
104	    '''
105	    num_stride_per_block: tl.constexpr = BLOCK_SIZE // K_STRIDE
106	    num_block_per_tile: tl.constexpr = K_TILE_SIZE // num_stride_per_block
107	
108	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
109	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
110	
111	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
112	    group_size = num_q_heads // num_k_heads
113	
114	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
115	    offset_k_heads = offset_q_heads // group_size
116	
117	    offset_q_ptr = offset_batch * stride_qz + offset_q_heads * stride_qh
118	    offset_k_ptr = offset_batch * stride_kz + offset_k_heads * stride_kh
119	
120	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + offset_q_ptr
121	    K_base_ptr = K_ptr + offset_k_ptr
122	
123	    offset_sc_ptr = offset_batch * stride_scz + offset_q_heads * stride_sch
124	    offset_mx_ptr = offset_batch * stride_mxz + offset_q_heads * stride_mxh
125	
126	    sc_base_ptr = sc_ptr + offset_sc_ptr
127	    mx_base_ptr = mx_ptr + offset_mx_ptr
128	
129	    offset_q = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE)
130	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
131	
132	    q_index_max = query_tile_index * BLOCK_SIZE + BLOCK_SIZE - 1
133	    q_index = offset_q
134	    
135	    q = tl.load(
136	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qm + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
137	        mask=(offset_q[:, None] < query_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD),
138	        other=0.0
139	    )
140	
141	    lo = 0
142	    hi = tl.cdiv(q_index_max, K_STRIDE)
143	
144	    sm_scale = scale * 1.4426950408889634
145	
146	    for j in range(lo, hi, K_TILE_SIZE):
147	        offset_k = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
148	        k_index_min = offset_k * K_STRIDE
149	        k_index_max = offset_k * K_STRIDE + K_STRIDE - 1
150	        k = tl.load(
151	            K_base_ptr + offset_k[:, None] * stride_kn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_kd,
152	            mask=(offset_k[:, None] < sub_key_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD),
153	            other=0.0
154	        )
155	
156	        qk = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k))
157	        causal_mask = (q_index[:, None] >= k_index_max[None, :]) # (block_size, k_tile_size)
158	
159	        qk = tl.where(causal_mask, qk, float('-inf'))
160	        qk *= sm_scale # (block_size, (num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
161	
162	        qk = tl.reshape(qk, (BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
163	        m_i_block = tl.max(qk, axis=2) #(block_size, num_block_per_tile)
164	        m_i_block = tl.max(m_i_block, axis=0) # (num_block_per_tile)
165	
166	        qk_block = qk - m_i_block[None, :, None]
167	        p_block = tl.exp2(qk_block)
168	
169	        causal_mask_block = tl.reshape(causal_mask, (BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block))
170	        p_block = tl.where(causal_mask_block, p_block, 0.0) #(BLOCK_SIZE, num_block_per_tile, num_stride_per_block)
171	
172	
173	        p_block = tl.sum(p_block, 2) 
174	        p_block = tl.sum(p_block, 0) # num_block_per_tile
175	
176	        offset_k_blocks = (j // K_TILE_SIZE) * num_block_per_tile + tl.arange(0, num_block_per_tile)
177	
178	        tl.store(
179	            sc_base_ptr + query_tile_index * stride_scmb + offset_k_blocks * stride_scnb,
180	            p_block,
181	            mask=(query_tile_index < tl.cdiv(query_len, BLOCK_SIZE)) & (offset_k_blocks < tl.cdiv(query_len, BLOCK_SIZE))
182	        )
183	
184	        tl.store(
185	            mx_base_ptr + query_tile_index * stride_mxmb + offset_k_blocks * stride_mxnb,
186	            m_i_block,
187	            mask=(query_tile_index < tl.cdiv(query_len, BLOCK_SIZE)) & (offset_k_blocks < tl.cdiv(query_len, BLOCK_SIZE))
188	        )
189	
190	
191	def get_configs():
192	    configs = []
193	    tile_sizes = [
194	        (64, 64),   
195	        (128, 64),  
196	        (64, 128)   
197	    ]
198	    warps_list = [4, 8]
199	    stages_list = [2, 3, 4, 5]
200	
201	    for (q_tile, k_tile) in tile_sizes:
202	        for w in warps_list:
203	            for s in stages_list:
204	                if q_tile == 128 and w == 4:
205	                    continue
206	                
207	                configs.append(
208	                    triton.Config(
209	                        {'Q_TILE_SIZE': q_tile, 'K_TILE_SIZE': k_tile}, 
210	                        num_warps=w, 
211	                        num_stages=s
212	                    )
213	                )
214	    return configs
215	
216	@triton.autotune(
217	    configs=get_configs(), 
218	    key=['query_len', 'key_len', 'num_q_heads', 'num_k_heads', 'BLOCK_SIZE'],
219	    prune_configs_by={
220	        'early_config_prune': lambda configs, named_args, **kwargs: [
221	            c for c in configs 
222	            if c.kwargs['Q_TILE_SIZE'] <= kwargs['BLOCK_SIZE'] 
223	            and c.kwargs['K_TILE_SIZE'] <= kwargs['BLOCK_SIZE']
224	        ]
225	    }
226	)
227	@triton.jit
228	def _flash_forward(
229	    Q_ptr, K_ptr, V_ptr, O_ptr, index_ptr, valid_ptr, scale,
230	    stride_qz, stride_qm, stride_qh, stride_qd,
231	    stride_kz, stride_kn, stride_kh, stride_kd,
232	    stride_vz, stride_vn, stride_vh, stride_vd,
233	    stride_oz, stride_om, stride_oh, stride_od,
234	    stride_indexz, stride_indexm, stride_indexn, stride_indexh,
235	    stride_validz, stride_validm, stride_validh,
236	    query_len, key_len,
237	    num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
238	    Q_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr, K_TILE_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
239	    BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr,
240	    D_HEAD: tl.constexpr,
241	):
242	    query_tile_index = tl.program_id(0).to(tl.int64)
243	    offset_zh = tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
244	
245	    offset_batch = offset_zh // num_q_heads
246	    group_size = num_q_heads // num_k_heads
247	    index_group_size = BLOCK_SIZE // Q_TILE_SIZE
248	    '''
249	    BLOCK_SIZE >= Q_TILE_SIZE
250	    BLOCK_SIZE >= K_TILE_SIZE
251	    only support key_len == query_len
252	    '''
253	    offset_q_heads = offset_zh % num_q_heads
254	    offset_k_heads = offset_q_heads // group_size
255	
256	    offset_q_ptr = offset_batch * stride_qz + offset_q_heads * stride_qh
257	    offset_k_ptr = offset_batch * stride_kz + offset_k_heads * stride_kh
258	    offset_v_ptr = offset_batch * stride_vz + offset_k_heads * stride_vh
259	    offset_o_ptr = offset_batch * stride_oz + offset_q_heads * stride_oh
260	    offset_index_ptr = offset_batch * stride_indexz + offset_q_heads * stride_indexh + (query_tile_index // index_group_size) * stride_indexm
261	    offset_valid_ptr = offset_batch * stride_validz + offset_q_heads * stride_validh + (query_tile_index // index_group_size) * stride_validm
262	
263	    Q_base_ptr = Q_ptr + offset_q_ptr
264	    K_base_ptr = K_ptr + offset_k_ptr
265	    V_base_ptr = V_ptr + offset_v_ptr
266	    O_base_ptr = O_ptr + offset_o_ptr
267	    index_base_ptr = index_ptr + offset_index_ptr
268	    valid_base_ptr = valid_ptr + offset_valid_ptr
269	
270	    offset_q = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
271	    offset_dim = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
272	
273	    q_mask = (offset_q[:, None] < query_len) & (offset_dim[None, :] < D_HEAD)
274	    q = tl.load(
275	        Q_base_ptr + offset_q[:, None] * stride_qm + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_qd,
276	        mask=q_mask,
277	        other=0.0
278	    ) 
279	
280	    sm_scale = scale * 1.4426950408889634
281	
282	    m_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), float("-inf"), dtype=tl.float32)
283	    l_i = tl.full((Q_TILE_SIZE,), 1.0, dtype=tl.float32)
284	    acc = tl.zeros((Q_TILE_SIZE, D_HEAD), dtype=tl.float32)
285	
286	    lo = 0
287	    hi = tl.load(valid_base_ptr)
288	
289	    block_index = tl.load(index_base_ptr + lo * stride_indexn)
290	
291	    for i in range(lo, hi):
292	        next_block_index = tl.load(index_base_ptr + (i+1) * stride_indexn, mask=(i+1<hi), other=0)
293	        key_start_index = block_index * BLOCK_SIZE
294	        key_end_index = (block_index + 1) * BLOCK_SIZE
295	
296	        is_diagonal_block = (block_index == (query_tile_index // index_group_size))
297	
298	        for j in range(key_start_index, key_end_index, K_TILE_SIZE):
299	            k_index = j + tl.arange(0, K_TILE_SIZE)
300	            k_j = tl.load(
301	                K_base_ptr + k_index[:, None] * stride_kn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_kd,
302	                mask=(k_index[:, None]<key_len) & (offset_dim[None, :]<D_HEAD),
303	                other=0.0
304	            )
305	            v_j = tl.load(
306	                V_base_ptr + k_index[:, None] * stride_vn + offset_dim[None, :] * stride_vd,
307	                mask=(k_index[:, None]<key_len) & (offset_dim[None, :]<D_HEAD),
308	                other=0.0
309	            )
310	
311	            qk = tl.dot(q, tl.trans(k_j))
312	            if is_diagonal_block:
313	                dist = offset_q[:, None] - k_index[None, :]
314	                causal_mask = (dist >= 0)
315	                qk = tl.where(causal_mask, qk, float('-inf'))
316	            qk *= sm_scale
317	            m_i_new = tl.maximum(m_i, tl.max(qk, axis=1))
318	            qk -= m_i_new[:, None]
319	            p = tl.exp2(qk) 
320	            # if is_diagonal_block:
321	            #     p = tl.where(causal_mask, p, 0.0)
322	            lij = tl.sum(p, 1)
323	            alpha = tl.exp2(m_i - m_i_new)
324	            alpha_mask = (alpha != alpha)
325	            alpha = tl.where(alpha_mask, 1.0, alpha)
326	            acc *= alpha[:, None]
327	            l_i = l_i * alpha + lij
328	            p = p.to(v_j.dtype) 
329	            acc += tl.dot(p, v_j)
330	            m_i = m_i_new
331	        block_index = next_block_index
332	
333	    l_rec = 1 / l_i[:, None]
334	    acc = acc * l_rec
335	
336	    o_offset_m = query_tile_index * Q_TILE_SIZE + tl.arange(0, Q_TILE_SIZE)
337	    o_offset_d = tl.arange(0, D_HEAD)
338	
339	    o_mask = (o_offset_m[:, None] < query_len) & (o_offset_d[None, :] < D_HEAD)
340	    tl.store(
341	        O_base_ptr + o_offset_m[:, None] * stride_om + o_offset_d[None, :] * stride_od,
342	        acc.to(q.dtype),
343	        mask=o_mask
344	    )
345	
346	
347	@torch.compile(mode="reduce-overhead")
348	def deal_output_score(score: torch.Tensor, attention_sink, window, alpha=0.1, last_n_blocks_full=2, min_budget=0):
349	    B, M, N, H = score.shape
350	    device = score.device
351	    k_ids = torch.arange(N, device=device).view(1, 1, N, 1)
352	
353	    if min_budget > 0:
354	        topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(score, k=min_budget, dim=2)
355	        max_val = topk_vals[:, :, :1, :] 
356	        mask_score = score >= (max_val * alpha)
357	        mask_score.scatter_(2, topk_indices, True) 
358	    else:
359	        max_val = score.max(dim=2, keepdim=True).values
360	        mask_score = score >= (max_val * alpha)
361	
362	    q_ids = torch.arange(M, device=device).view(1, M, 1, 1)
363	    
364	
365	    mask_sink = (k_ids < attention_sink)
366	    
367	    dist = q_ids - k_ids
368	    mask_window = (dist >= 0) & (dist < window)
369	    
370	    mask_last_full = (q_ids >= (M - last_n_blocks_full))
371	
372	    mask_causal = (dist >= 0)
373	
374	    is_active = mask_score | mask_sink | mask_window | mask_last_full
375	    is_active = is_active & mask_causal
376	
377	    counts = is_active.sum(dim=2).int()
378	
379	    indices = k_ids.expand(B, M, N, H)
380	
381	    indices_to_sort = indices.masked_fill(~is_active, N)
382	
383	    compact_indices, _ = indices_to_sort.sort(dim=2)
384	        
385	    return compact_indices, counts
386	
387	
388	@torch.compile
389	def normalize_scores(output_score: torch.Tensor, output_max: torch.Tensor):
390	
391	    mask = (output_max != float('-inf'))
392	
393	    max_in_max = torch.max(output_max, dim=2, keepdim=True).values
394	    output_max = output_max - max_in_max
395	    output_max = torch.exp2(output_max)
396	    output_max = torch.where(mask, output_max, 1.0)
397	    output_score = torch.where(mask, output_score, 0.0)
398	
399	    output_score = output_score * output_max
400	
401	    output_score = (output_score ) / (output_score.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)
402	
403	    return output_score    
404	
405	class FlashPrefill(torch.autograd.Function):
406	
407	    @staticmethod
408	    @contiguous
409	    @autocast_custom_fwd
410	    def forward(ctx, q, k, v, out, block_size=128, k_stride=128, attention_sink=2, window=4, alpha=0.7, last_n_blocks_full=2, n_uniform=0, min_buget=0, topk=0):
411	        '''
412	        q: (b s nq d)
413	        k, v: (b s nk d)
414	        '''
415	        assert block_size == k_stride
416	        batch_size, seq_len, num_q_heads, head_dim = q.shape
417	        num_k_heads = k.shape[-2]
418	
419	        mean_k = torch.empty(batch_size, triton.cdiv(seq_len, k_stride), num_k_heads, head_dim, dtype=k.dtype, device=k.device)
420	
421	        def grid_mean(meta):
422	            return (triton.cdiv(seq_len, k_stride), batch_size * num_k_heads, 1)
423	        compute_mean_vector[grid_mean](
424	            k, mean_k,
425	            *k.stride(),
426	            *mean_k.stride(),
427	            num_k_heads,
428	            seq_len,
429	            k_stride,
430	            head_dim
431	        )
432	
433	        output_score = torch.full(size=(batch_size, triton.cdiv(seq_len, block_size), triton.cdiv(seq_len, block_size), num_q_heads), fill_value=float('-inf'), dtype=torch.float32, device=q.device)
434	        output_max = torch.full(size=(batch_size, triton.cdiv(seq_len, block_size), triton.cdiv(seq_len, block_size), num_q_heads), fill_value=float('-inf'), dtype=torch.float32, device=q.device)
435	
436	        def grid_score(meta):
437	            return (triton.cdiv(seq_len, block_size), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
438	        compute_block_score[grid_score](
439	            q, mean_k, 1 / (head_dim ** 0.5), output_score, output_max,
440	            *q.stride(),
441	            *mean_k.stride(),
442	            *output_score.stride(),
443	            *output_max.stride(),
444	            num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
445	            seq_len, triton.cdiv(seq_len, k_stride),
446	            block_size,
447	            K_STRIDE=k_stride,
448	            D_HEAD=head_dim
449	        )
450	
451	
452	        output_score = normalize_scores(output_score, output_max)
453	
454	        block_index, counts = deal_output_score(output_score, attention_sink, window, alpha, last_n_blocks_full, min_buget) # formal use
455	        # block_index, counts = random_mask_indices(output_score, 0.041) # efficiency test
456	
457	        # total, activate = static_port(block_index, counts)
458	
459	        def grid(meta):
460	            return (triton.cdiv(seq_len, meta['Q_TILE_SIZE']), batch_size * num_q_heads, 1)
461	
462	        # print(block_index.shape)
463	
464	        _flash_forward[grid](
465	            q, k, v, out, block_index, counts, 1 / (head_dim ** 0.5),
466	            *q.stride(),
467	            *k.stride(),
468	            *v.stride(),
469	            *out.stride(),
470	            *block_index.stride(),
471	            *counts.stride(),
472	            seq_len, seq_len,
473	            num_q_heads, num_k_heads,
474	            BLOCK_SIZE=block_size,
475	            D_HEAD=head_dim,
476	        )
477	
478	
479	
480	@torch.no_grad()
481	def flash_prefill(
482	    q: torch.Tensor,
483	    k: torch.Tensor,
484	    v: torch.Tensor,
485	    out: torch.Tensor,
486	    block_size: int,
487	    attention_sink: int,
488	    window_size: int,
489	    alpha: float,
490	    last_n_block_full: int,
491	    min_budget: int
492	):
493	    '''
494	    q, k, v: (b h s d)
495	    '''
496	    FlashPrefill.apply(
497	        q, k, v, out, block_size, block_size, attention_sink, window_size,
498	        alpha, last_n_block_full, 0, min_budget, 0
499	    )
500
```

```
21	def qwen2_5_vl_llm_attention_forward(
22	    self,
23	    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
24	    attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
25	    position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
26	    past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,

31	    **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],
32	):
33	    bsz, q_len, _ = hidden_states.size()
34

36	    key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states)

46	    )

51	
52	    
53	
54	    if query_states.shape[2] == key_states.shape[2]:

64	    else:
65	        attn_output, attn_weights = flash_attention_forward(
66	            self,
67	            query_states,
68	            key_states,
69	            value_states,
70	            attention_mask,
71	            dropout=0.0 if not self.training else self.attention_dropout,
72	            scaling=self.scaling,
73	            sliding_window=self.sliding_window,
74	            position_ids=position_ids,  # pass positions for FA2
75	            **kwargs,
76	        )
77	    
78	    attn_output = attn_output.reshape(bsz, q_len, -1).contiguous()
79	    attn_output = self.o_proj(attn_output)
80	    return attn_output, attn_weights #, total_blocks, activated_blocks
81	
82	def qwen2_5_vl_decoder_forward(
83	    self,
84	    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
85	    attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
86	    position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
87	    past_key_values: Optional[tuple[torch.Tensor]] = None,
88	    output_attentions: Optional[bool] = False,
89	    use_cache: Optional[bool] = False,
90	    cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
91	    position_embeddings: Optional[tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = None,  # necessary, but kept here for BC
92	    **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],
93	):
94	    residual = hidden_states
95	
96	    hidden_states = self.input_layernorm(hidden_states)
97	
98	    # Self Attention
99	    hidden_states, self_attn_weights = self.self_attn(
100	        hidden_states=hidden_states,
101	        attention_mask=attention_mask,
102	        position_ids=position_ids,
103	        past_key_values=past_key_values,
104	        output_attentions=output_attentions,
105	        use_cache=use_cache,
106	        cache_position=cache_position,
107	        position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
108	        **kwargs,
109	    )
110	    hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
111	
112	    residual = hidden_states
113	    hidden_states = self.post_attention_layernorm(hidden_states)
114	    hidden_states = self.mlp(hidden_states)
115	    hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
116	
117	    outputs = (hidden_states,)
118	
119	    if output_attentions:
120	        outputs += (self_attn_weights,)
121	
122	    return outputs
123	
124	def qwen2_5_text_model_forward(
125	    self,
126	    input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
127	    attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
128	    position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
129	    past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,
130	    inputs_embeds: Optional[torch.FloatTensor] = None,
131	    use_cache: Optional[bool] = None,
132	    output_attentions: Optional[bool] = None,
133	    output_hidden_states: Optional[bool] = None,
134	    return_dict: Optional[bool] = None,
135	    cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,
136	    **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],
137	):
138	    output_attentions = output_attentions if output_attentions is not None else self.config.output_attentions
139	    output_hidden_states = (
140	        output_hidden_states if output_hidden_states is not None else self.config.output_hidden_states
141	    )
142	    use_cache = use_cache if use_cache is not None else self.config.use_cache
143	
144	    return_dict = return_dict if return_dict is not None else self.config.use_return_dict
145	
146	    if (input_ids is None) ^ (inputs_embeds is not None):
147	        raise ValueError("You must specify exactly one of input_ids or inputs_embeds")
148	
149	    if self.gradient_checkpointing and self.training:
150	        if use_cache:
151	            logger.warning_once(
152	                "`use_cache=True` is incompatible with gradient checkpointing. Setting `use_cache=False`..."
153	            )
154	            use_cache = False
155	
156	    # torch.jit.trace() doesn't support cache objects in the output
157	    if use_cache and past_key_values is None and not torch.jit.is_tracing():
158	        past_key_values = DynamicCache(config=self.config)
159	
160	    if inputs_embeds is None:
161	        inputs_embeds = self.embed_tokens(input_ids)
162	
163	    if cache_position is None:
164	        past_seen_tokens = past_key_values.get_seq_length() if past_key_values is not None else 0
165	        cache_position = torch.arange(
166	            past_seen_tokens, past_seen_tokens + inputs_embeds.shape[1], device=inputs_embeds.device
167	        )
168	
169	    # the hard coded `3` is for temporal, height and width.
170	    if position_ids is None:
171	        position_ids = cache_position.view(1, 1, -1).expand(3, inputs_embeds.shape[0], -1)
172	    elif position_ids.ndim == 2:
173	        position_ids = position_ids[None, ...].expand(3, position_ids.shape[0], -1)
174	
175	    # NOTE: we need to pass text position ids for packing. Qwen2-VL uses 3D positions
176	    # where each dim indicates visual spatial positions for temporal/height/width grids.
177	    # There are two scenarios when FA2-like packed masking might be activated.
178	    # 1. User specifically passed packed `position_ids` and no attention mask.
179	    #    In this case we expect the useer to create correct position ids for all 3 grids
180	    #    and prepend text-only position ids to it. The final tensor will be [4, bs, seq-len]
181	    # 2. User runs forward with no attention mask and no position ids. In this case, position ids
182	    #    are prepared by the model (`get_rope_index`) as `[4, bs, seq-len]` tensor. Text-only positions are
183	    #    prepended by us when creating positions so that the mask is constructed correctly. NOTE: failing to pass
184	    #    text-only positions will cause incorrect mask construction, do not change `prepare_input_for_generation`
185	    if position_ids.ndim == 3 and position_ids.shape[0] == 4:
186	        text_position_ids = position_ids[0]
187	        position_ids = position_ids[1:]
188	    else:
189	        text_position_ids = position_ids[0]
190	
191	
192	    hidden_states = inputs_embeds
193	
194	    # create position embeddings to be shared across the decoder layers
195	    position_embeddings = self.rotary_emb(hidden_states, position_ids)
196	
197	    # decoder layers
198	    all_hidden_states = () if output_hidden_states else None
199	    all_self_attns = () if output_attentions else None
200	
201	
202	    for decoder_layer in self.layers:
203	        if output_hidden_states:
204	            all_hidden_states += (hidden_states,)
205	
206	        layer_outputs = decoder_layer(
207	            hidden_states,
208	            attention_mask=None,
209	            position_ids=text_position_ids,
210	            past_key_values=past_key_values,
211	            output_attentions=output_attentions,
212	            use_cache=use_cache,
213	            cache_position=cache_position,
214	            position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
215	            **kwargs,
216	        )
217	
218	        hidden_states = layer_outputs[0]
219	
220	
221	        if output_attentions:
222	            all_self_attns += (layer_outputs[1],)
223	    
224	
225	    hidden_states = self.norm(hidden_states)
226	
227	    # add hidden states from the last decoder layer
228	    if output_hidden_states:
229	        all_hidden_states += (hidden_states,)
230	
231	    if not return_dict:
232	        return tuple(
233	            v for v in [hidden_states, past_key_values, all_hidden_states, all_self_attns] if v is not None
234	        )
235	    return BaseModelOutputWithPast(
236	        last_hidden_state=hidden_states,
237	        past_key_values=past_key_values,
238	        hidden_states=all_hidden_states,
239	        attentions=all_self_attns,
240	    )
241	
242	def patch_qwen2_5_vl_model(model: Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration):
243	    from transformers.models.qwen2_5_vl.modeling_qwen2_5_vl import (
244	        Qwen2_5_VLAttention,
245	        Qwen2_5_VLDecoderLayer,
246	        Qwen2_5_VLTextModel
247	    )
248	
249	    assert isinstance(model, Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration)
250	
251	    for idx, m in model.named_modules():
252	        if isinstance(m, Qwen2_5_VLAttention):
253	            m.forward = types.MethodType(qwen2_5_vl_llm_attention_forward, m)
254	        elif isinstance(m, Qwen2_5_VLDecoderLayer):
255	            m.forward = types.MethodType(qwen2_5_vl_decoder_forward, m)
256	        elif isinstance(m, Qwen2_5_VLTextModel):
257	            m.forward = types.MethodType(qwen2_5_text_model_forward, m)
258
```

> AGENT

读完了 `/tmp/FlashPrefill/`（README + `ops/flashprefill_native_forward.py` + `ops/flashprefill_varlen_func.py` + `patches/patch_qwen2_5_vl.py`）。先讲它是什么，再对比我们 stack，最后给结论。

## FlashPrefill 在做什么

训练-free 的 dynamic block-sparse prefill，4-stage Triton 流水线：

1. `compute_mean_vector` — 每个 K block 取均值，得到 block representative（类似 InfLLM-v2 的 compress_k）
2. `compute_block_score` — Q × mean_K → 近似 attention score；同时记录每个 block 的 max
3. `compute_block_sub_max` + `compute_block_max` — **核心卖点 dynamic threshold**：`sc >= alpha * max(sc)` 直接筛 block，**不排序、不 top-K、不 cumsum**；再加 attention sink + sliding window + last_n_full + causal mask
4. `_flash_block_sparse_varlen` — 用稀疏 block index 跑 FA-style 输出

论文 27.78× 是对比 **dense FA**（256K seq），4K 还有 1.71×；引用 MInference / FlexPrefill / XAttention 这条线。

## 与我们 stack 的对应关系

| 阶段 | FlashPrefill | InfLLM-v2（standard attn, dense_len>8192） |
|---|---|---|
| K 压缩 | 简单 mean（训练-free） | compress_k（**模型架构一部分**，不是随便 mean） |
| block 打分 | Q × mean_K + max | stage1 block_score |
| block 选择 | `sc >= α·max`（threshold） | **top-K** |
| sparse FA | Triton block-sparse varlen | FlashInfer stage2 sparse FA |

结构上几乎一对一，但语义不一样。

## 对我们能不能帮上忙

**主要 blocker**：

1. **基座不可替换** — InfLLM-v2 的 compress_k 是 SALA 训练里定的 reduce（不是简单 mean），block selection 是 top-K。把 compress_k 换成 mean、把 top-K 换成 threshold = 改 attention 算子语义，精度风险极高。这是 critical rule。

2. **覆盖面错位** — FlashPrefill 只解 standard attention long-context。我们 32 层里只有 8 层 standard、24 层 GLA；GLA 那边它压根不管。即便 standard 那 8 层全切，prefill 总收益封顶很有限。

3. **Triton vs FlashInfer** — sm_120 上我们 stage2 sparse FA 跑的是 FlashInfer 0.6.8（cu13 编译），FlashPrefill 是纯 Triton。同样稀疏度下 Triton 大概率慢。它论文比的 baseline 是 dense FA，不是我们已经在跑的 sparse stack。

**可能的局部启发**（不需要整套集成）：

- **dynamic threshold 替代 top-K 的 ablation 想法**：在 InfLLM-v2 stage1 block_score 出来后，把 top-K 改成 `score >= α·max` 的 threshold 选块，理论上能利用长尾分布做出更高稀疏度。**但** 这等于改 attention pattern，需要全量精度回归（mcq + 长上下文 needle），而且 SALA 是带着 top-K 训练的，threshold 出来的分布未必兼容。是个研究题，不是工程优化。
- `compute_block_score` 里 `BLOCK_SIZE / num_block_per_tile / num_stride_per_block` 的 reshape 技巧（一次 dot 出 N 个 sub-block 的 max + sum）写得不错，如果哪天要重写 stage1 kernel 可以参考。

## 结论

**不建议 wholesale 集成**：算子语义和我们模型训练假设冲突，且只覆盖 25% 的 layer，剩下 GLA 不解决。论文 27.78× 是 vs dense baseline，我们 baseline 是 sparse，搬不过来。

**值得做的事**：把"dynamic threshold 替代 top-K"作为单点 ablation 实验（只在 stage1 后改 selection），先看精度退多少、稀疏度涨多少，再决定要不要继续。如果你想推，我可以在 `eagle/` 之外开个 `probe-sala-flashprefill/` 做隔离实验。

人话：方向有意思，但不是我们当前 prefill 的对症药。

1	# Prefill 优化当前事实
2	
3	目标场景：长上下文 prefill，`--chunked-prefill-size 8192`，`--dense-as-sparse`，`minicpm_flashinfer`。线上快速样本：`bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` line 39 / index 39，约 15 chunks，`max_tokens=1`。最终大 bench：

8	
9	## 1. 当前热点
10	
11	profile 口径：`SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1 SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL=16`。
12	
13	最新真实 15chunk full-chunk profile（line 39，`mars=0`，`max_tokens=1`）：
14	
15	| 模块 | 现象 |
16	|---|---|
17	| MLP | `133ms/chunk`：`gate_up≈70ms` + `act_quant_down_fused≈63ms`（已 fuse） |
18	| GLA | `120ms/chunk`：`gla_qkv≈45ms` + `gla_chunk_kernel≈27-30ms` |
19	| `extend_sparse_fa` | 8 sparse layer × `9.8-10.0ms`，`84ms/chunk` |
20	| `stage1_score` | 末段 `74ms/chunk`，离线单层 ctx=131072 ≈ `10.38ms` |
21	| FlashInfer plan / sparse metadata | layer/chunk cache 后压平，残余主要是首 chunk + 尾 shape |
22	
23	15chunk wall 当前稳态约 **7.0-7.4 s**（`max_tokens=1`）。
24	
25	**硬边界**：不能再把 topk/candidate 缩限作为优化方向。任何减少候选集合、截断 topk、近似 fused topk 默认不满足一致性，除非先证明它与完整语义逐 token/topk 完全一致。
26	
27	## 2. 已落地默认优化
28	
29	每条均默认开启；env 是回退/调试旋钮。
30	
31	### 2.1 FlashInfer plan cache
32	
33	代码：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_attention_kernels.py`
34	
35	| env | 默认 | 作用 |
36	|---|---:|---|
37	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE` | `1` | 启用 sparse FA plan 复用 |
38	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_DISABLE_CROSS_CHUNK_PLAN_CACHE` | `0` | 1=禁跨 chunk，仅保留 layer 内 |
39	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_CROSS_FORWARD_BUFFER_REFRESH` | `1` | 跨 forward 命中时刷新 wrapper page-table buffer，不重新 plan |
40	
41	关键语义：跨 forward 复用 plan 时必须刷新 `wrapper._paged_kv_indptr_buf` / `_paged_kv_indices_buf` / `_paged_kv_last_page_len_buf`，否则 stale page table 会导致 cross-request KV contamination。
42	
43	### 2.2 全 sparse metadata 快路径
44	
45	代码：`minicpm_sparse_utils.py`
46	
47	| env | 默认 |
48	|---|---:|
49	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_ALL_SPARSE_PREFILL_METADATA` | `1` |
50	
51	仅当 `len(sparse_bs_list) == bs` 启用：`sparse_cu_seqlens_q_cpu = arange`，`sparse_page_table = empty`（后续整张覆盖），token mapping 向量化，page-table 写用 slice 替换高级索引。混合 batch 走原逻辑。
52	
53	### 2.3 sparse seqlens 从 topk 派生
54	
55	代码：`minicpm_backend.py`
56	
57	| env | 默认 |
58	|---|---:|
59	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_DERIVED_SPARSE_SEQLENS` | `1` |
60	
61	不能固定成 `num_sparse_topk_tokens`（early token 会被 causal 截断）。按 `topk_idx` / `token_pos_in_bs` / `seqlen_k_sparse_bs` / `block_size` 复现 `get_block_table_v2` 的有效长度：
62	
63	```python
64	limit = min(seqlen_k_sparse_bs[token_bs], token_pos_in_bs)
65	valid_len = clamp(limit - topk_idx * block_size, 0, block_size)
66	sparse_seqlen = sum(valid_len over topk blocks)
67	```

70	
71	### 2.4 direct sparse page table
72

74	
75	| env | 默认 |
76	|---|---:|
77	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_DIRECT_SPARSE_PAGE_TABLE` | `1` |
78	
79	全 sparse 下 `sparse_kernel_extension.get_block_table_*` 输出已是完整 page table，跳过 `metadata.sparse_page_table[...] = ...` 复制。混合 batch 仍走原合并。
80	
81	### 2.5 stage1 actual maxlen + direct pool + skip extra zero
82	
83	代码：`minicpm_sparse_utils.py`
84	
85	| env | 默认 | 作用 |
86	|---|---:|---|
87	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE1_ACTUAL_MAXLEN` | `1` | `infllmv2_attn_stage1` 用实际 K1 长度 |
88	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE1_DIRECT_POOL` | `1` | actual score 覆盖 topk 候选时直接 pool/topk |
89	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE1_SKIP_EXTRA_ZERO` | `1` | 跳过 wrapper 对 score tensor 的二次 `p.zero_()` |
90	
91	边界：
92	
93	- 可以缩 `infllmv2_attn_stage1.max_seqlen_k`。
94	- **不能**缩 `max_pooling_1d_varlen.max_context_len`：旧 `compressed_max_seqlen_k` 精度问题来自 pooler layout/边界语义。
95	- 首 chunk / early chunk 候选不足时必须回到 full-layout scratch。

100	
101	| env | 默认 |

103	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_PREFILL_BLOCK_TABLE_V3` | `1` |
104	
105	guard：仅当 `min(seq_lens_cpu) >= num_sparse_topk_tokens` 启用。`get_block_table_v3` 没有 `topk_idx=-1` guard，带 `-1` 会 CUDA illegal memory access。profile 上 `sparse_block_table_prefill_ms` 12.5→8.8ms/chunk。

109	| env | 默认 | 作用 |
110	|---|---:|---|
111	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_POOL_EMPTY_OUTPUT` | `1` | pool 输出用 `empty`（kernel 写满每元素） |
112	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_TOPK_UNSORTED_SELECT` | `1` | `topk(..., sorted=False).indices.sort()` |
113

116	### 2.8 GLA qkv cuDNN shape gate
117	
118	代码：`modelopt_quant.py`
119	
120	| env | 默认 | 作用 |
121	|---|---:|---|
122	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_FP4_GLA_QKV_CUDNN` | `1` | GLA qkv 命中 shape 走 `mm_fp4(backend=cudnn)` |
123	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_FP4_GLA_QKV_CUDNN_M` | `8192` | 仅完整 chunk M 命中 |
124	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_PREWARM_FP4_GLA_QKV_CUDNN` | `1` | 启动时预热一次 `M=8192,K=4096,N=12288` |
125	
126	边界：
127	
128	- 全局 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 已否决（MLP gate_up 变慢）。
129	- `M=8192,K=4096,N=12288` 离线 `max_diff=0.0`；`M=2754` 离线 `max_diff=362.0`，所以 gate 收紧到 `x_m == 8192`。
130	- 未预热时首个 full chunk `gla_qkv_proj_ms≈200ms`（cudnn handle/tactic 初始化）；预热后挪到 startup。
131	
132	收益：full chunk `gla_qkv_proj_ms` 77-80→43-45ms；15chunk wall **+2.4%**。
133	
134	### 2.9 MLP fused SiluAndMul + FP4 quant
135	
136	`sgl_kernel.silu_and_mul_scaled_fp4_grouped_quant` 把 `silu_and_mul + fp4 quant` 合成一个 kernel，直接喂 NVFP4 GEMM。
137	
138	| env | 默认 |
139	|---|---:|
140	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_MLP_ACT_QUANT` | `1` |
141	
142	guard：CUDA、2D contiguous gate_up、无 bias、无 `pre_quant_scale`、不走 Marlin 小 M。
143	
144	收益：full chunk `mlp_ms` 148-151→133-138ms；15chunk wall **+1.5%**。
145	
146	验证：离线 `M=8192,K=16384,N=4096` `out_equal=True max_diff=0`；smoke 中文输出正常。
147	
148	### 2.10 direct topk → FlashInfer indices
149	
150	代码：`minicpm_sparse_stage2.py::triton_topk_to_flashinfer_indices` + `minicpm_backend.py`
151	
152	| env | 默认 |
153	|---|---:|
154	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_TOPK_TO_FI_INDICES` | `1` |
155	
156	绕过 metadata 中转：`topk_idx → triton_fused → kv_indptr/indices/lpl`，跳过 `sparse_page_table` materialize/write。guard：全 sparse + full-topk + `block_size=64`。`kv_indptr/indices/lpl` 改为持久化 `empty` buffer，有效区由 `kv_indptr` 限定。
157	
158	收益：metadata microbench `~8x`；profile `sparse_block_table_prefill_ms=0.000`，`fi_convert_ms` 5.2→0.6ms/call；15chunk wall **+3-4%**。
159	
160	验证：synthetic `indptr_bad=0 indices_bad_valid=0 lpl_bad=0`；线上 debug check `bad=0`。
