162	### 2.11 InfLLM-v2 stage1 full-chunk trait guard
163	
164	代码：`kernels/infllmv2_cuda_impl/csrc/flash_attn/src/flash_fwd_launch_template.h`
165	
166	dispatch 改动：保留原 `16x64,1w`；仅当 `seqlen_q % 32 == 0 && total_q == seqlen_q * b`（同长 full chunk + adjusted-q 对齐）走 `32x64,2w`。tail / mixed-q batch 全部 fallback。
167	
168	不是 topk 缩限，stage1 仍完整算 `k1+k2`。最早全局换 `32x64` 在 `T=2747,K=8192` tail 出现 `diff_max=0.0046` 且 topk 不一致，必须有 guard。
169	
170	落地物：`demo-sala/prebuilt/infllm_v2_C.cpython-310-x86_64-linux-gnu.so`（`prepare_env.sh` G3 复制到 venv）；备份 `outputs/so_backups/infllm_v2_C.before_stage1_guard_389ead90.so`。
171	
172	验证：
173	
174	| shape | baseline | guard | result |
175	|---|---:|---:|---|
176	| `T=8192,K=8192` | `16.24ms` | `8.94ms` | bitwise equal, topk@128 equal |
177	| `T=2747,K=8192` | `5.62ms` | `5.64ms` | fallback, bitwise equal |
178	
179	线上 15chunk：guard `6.7587 / 6.6301 / 6.6119s` vs old `6.9956 / 6.8444 / 6.8100s`。
180	
181	补充复查：`64x64,4w` / `128x64,4w` 离线仍 bitwise/topk 一致但更慢；`128x64` 真实 15chunk 与 `32x64` 端到端打平。**stage1 trait sweep 已枯竭**，下一步必须是算法/语义级改动。
182	
183	### 2.12 GLA fused qk_norm + rope (TRT-LLM in-place)
184	
185	代码：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py::MiniCPMLightningMixer.forward`
186	
187	| env | 默认 | 作用 |
188	|---|---:|---|
189	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE` | `0` | 启用 sgl_kernel.fused_qk_norm_rope (TRT-LLM warp-per-(token,head))，in-place |
190	
191	替代 baseline 3 kernel：`sgl_kernel.rmsnorm(q) + sgl_kernel.rmsnorm(k) + apply_rope_with_cos_sin_cache_inplace`。
192	
193	guard：qk_norm + rope 同时启用、`num_heads == num_kv_heads` (lightning 32/32 multi-head)、head_dim ∈ {64,128,256}、no yarn (`rope_scaling=None`)、`is_neox_style=True`、`rotary_dim == head_dim`、qkv bf16 cuda contig.
194	
195	注意：in-place 修改 qkv，后续需 `qkv.split + 3x .contiguous()` 因为 chunk_simple_gla 在 strided (T,NH,D) 上走慢路径 (microbench fla NaN, prod +7ms gla_kernel)。
196	
197	数值：
198	- microbench (T=8192, NH=32, D=128) baseline 1044us / layer → fused pure 253us / layer
199	- 与 fp64 reference: fused 1.13 ULP < baseline 2.00 ULP — **fused 比 baseline 更接近真值**（warp-only fp32 reduce vs sgl_rmsnorm block-reduce）
200	- fused vs baseline: 2 ULP（bf16 round 重排不可避免最小差）
201	
202	线上 PROFILE=1 full chunk:
203	- attn_gla_ms 124.7 → 108.7 (-16ms / chunk)
204	- gla_qkv_split (post-kernel contig copies) 0.01 → 8.74
205	
206	**OOP 变体（§2.13）严格优于此 in-place 版本**，本条仅作 fallback / 数值对照。
207	
208	### 2.13 GLA fused qk_norm + rope (triton OOP — 推荐)
209	
210	代码：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py::_minicpm_fused_qknorm_rope_oop`
211	
212	| env | 默认 |
213	|---|---:|
214	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP` | `1` （已默认开启） |
215	
216	triton kernel 读 qkv (T, total_heads*D) read-only，写 q_out / k_out / v_out 三个独立 contig (T, NH, D) 输出 — 一个 launch 内同时做 Q rmsnorm+rope / K rmsnorm+rope / V passthrough。**直接消除 §2.12 的 split + 3x .contiguous() 8.74ms 开销**。
217	
218	接口：
219	- input: qkv bf16 contig (T, 3*NH*D)，q_w/k_w bf16 (D,)，cos_sin_cache fp32 (max_pos, D) [cos|sin] flat，positions int32 (T,)
220	- output: q_out/k_out/v_out bf16 contig (T, NH, D)
221	- 配置：BLOCK_M=16 num_warps=2 (sweep 后最佳)
222	
223	数值：
224	- microbench (T=8192, NH=32, D=128) ref 910us → 318us / layer，**24 layers 节省 ~14ms / chunk**
225	- vs ref (sgl in-place + split + contig): q 2 ULP, k 1 ULP, v 0 ULP (V 是纯 copy bitwise)
226	
227	线上 PROFILE=1 full chunk:
228	- gla_fused_qk_norm_rope_ms 7.22ms / chunk
229	- gla_qkv_split_ms 0 (contig copy 完全消失 vs §2.12 的 8.74ms)
230	- attn_gla_ms 124.7 → 108.7 → 96.4 (-28ms / chunk vs baseline)
231	
232	线上 wall (5x line 39, PROFILE=0)：med 6.830s → 6.794s（-36ms vs baseline）。
233	
234	### 2.14 GLA fused o_norm + sigmoid + mul (3-in-1 triton)
235	
236	代码：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py::_minicpm_gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul`
237	
238	| env | 默认 |
239	|---|---:|
240	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL` | `1` （已默认开启） |
241	| `SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_SIGMOID_MUL` | `0` （仅 sigmoid+mul 子集，被本条覆盖） |
242	
243	替代：
244	```python
245	# 原: 3 kernel
246	o = self.o_norm(o)        # sgl_kernel.rmsnorm
247	z = self.z_proj(...)
248	o = o * F.sigmoid(z)      # 2 kernels: sigmoid + mul
249	```
250	
251	合一 triton kernel 全 fp32 计算：
252	```
253	m[i,j] = o[i,j] * rstd[i] * w[j] * sigmoid(z[i,j])
254	```
255	重排安全：z_proj 只依赖 hidden_states，与 o 独立。
256	
257	数值：
258	- microbench (M=8192, H=4096, BLOCK_M=4 num_warps=4) ref 225us → 156us / call
259	- vs ref: 2 bf16 ULP（fp32 全程比 baseline 的 bf16 中间 cast 更精确方向）
260	
261	线上 PROFILE=1：`o_norm 1.96 + sigmoid_mul 2.44 = 4.4 ms` → `o_norm_sigmoid_mul 3.47 ms` (-1ms / chunk)。
262	
263	### 2.15 GLA 优化累计
264	
265	| 阶段 | min | med | mean(last4) |
266	|---|---:|---:|---:|
267	| baseline | 6.818 | 6.830 | 6.825 |
268	| in-place fused (§2.12) | 6.799 | 6.810 | 6.806 |
269	| OOP fused (§2.13) | 6.785 | 6.794 | 6.793 |
270	| + sigmoid_mul (§2.14a) | 6.778 | 6.787 | 6.792 |
271	| **+ rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul (§2.14)** | **6.772** | **6.775** | **6.783** |
272	
273	vs baseline: med −55ms / 117K-token line 39 (-0.81%)，mean -42ms。
274	PROFILE=1 GLA 段累计：123 → 96 ms / chunk (-27ms / chunk × 15 chunks = -405ms cuda time，但 wall 受 GPU clock / launch queue noise 稀释)。
275	
276	**chunk_simple_gla 27.6ms 是 GLA 不可压缩下限**：fla `chunk_h + chunk_o` 都已 BW-bound (BK=64 BV=64 sweep 211/317us 离线 vs prod 1004us — 1004us 是 prod GPU 真实 time，BKV_LIST 强制 [64] 实测稳态无收益)。
277	
278	## 3. 已否决路线
279	
280	每条记录：做了什么 / 为什么失败 / 不要再做什么。
281	
282	### 3.1 fi_convert 跨层缓存
283	
284	旧结论"同 forward 8 层 page table 相同"是错的：tensor 指针相同，但每个 standard layer 都按本层 q/k 重写内容。仅保留 `SGLANG_MINICPM_UNSAFE_FI_CONVERT_CACHE=1` 实验开关，不可线上。
285	
286	### 3.2 TrtLLM stage2 替换
287	
288	长上下文 sparse stage2 实际是 decode-style（`max_seqlen_q==1`）；FlashInfer causal 是 right-aligned，TrtLLM `mask_mode="causal"` 在 `q_len << kv_len` 时语义不等价。HEAD 已不含 `_USE_TRTLLM_STAGE2`。除非先做 right-aligned causal 等价的离线证明，否则不要继续。
289	
290	### 3.3 fast prefill stage1
291	
292	`SGLANG_FAST_PREFILL_STAGE1=1` 默认关闭。当前实现 k1-only 丢 k2 语义，没有完整复现官方 causal/full-layout pooler，topk match 差且不快。
293	
294	### 3.4 compressed maxlen 旧方案
295	
296	不能缩 `max_pooling_1d_varlen.max_context_len` / score full layout。精度根因在 pooler layout/边界语义，topk 放大后会改变 sparse blocks。当前可用的是 §2.5 stage1 actual maxlen + full-layout scratch/direct-pool guard。
297	
298	### 3.5 compressed-K buffer 复用
299	
300	缓存 `compressed_k/k2` 后 `fill_(-inf)` 实测 15chunk 打平（7.609 vs 7.602s），`compress_k_buffer_prefill_ms≈0.03ms/call`。不值得维护成本。
301	
302	### 3.6 `--fuse-topk`
303	
304	global 启用导致 target CUDA graph capture 6.5→14.4s，`decode_topk_ms` 跳到 16ms，运行中触发 CUDA illegal memory access（栈在 FlashInfer decode forward 后 synchronize）。是 decode/verify 路径风险，不是 prefill 优化。
305	
306	prefill-only 复查：`compressed_attention_tilelang` vs `compressed_attention` topk 大面积不匹配（如 `bad_sorted=19315/24576`），TileLang 只吃 `k1` 没复现 `k2/v` 语义。除非重写一个真正复现 stage1+k2 的 kernel，否则禁用。
307	
308	### 3.7 MLP cuDNN（down-only / full-chunk）
309	
310	- down 单独 cudnn：`max_diff=0` 但 `mlp_down_ms` 49→52-56ms（更慢）。
311	- 整 MLP `M=8192` cudnn：`gate/up/down max_diff=0`，但非 power-of-two M 出现明显 diff（`M=2754/6144`），且 `M=8192` e2e `down` 常更慢。
312	- `auto` 后端：tail M 选与 cutlass 不一致路径。
313	
314	不要新增 MLP cuDNN shape gate。
315	
316	### 3.8 stage1 q repeat
317	
318	`current_ratio < 16` 的 q repeat 在生产路径不触发（实测 `q_heads=32 kv_heads=2` ratio=16），跳过 repeat 的开关是 no-op。
319	
320	### 3.9 MLP activation buffer cache
321	
322	给 `SiluAndMul` 输出加全局 buffer，`mlp_act_ms` 不变，wall 无改善，还多占显存。
323	
324	### 3.10 跨层 topk 复用
325	
326	15chunk full chunk 相邻 standard sparse layer `ret` 完全一致率：首个 8K chunk 0.887-0.918，第二个起 0.423-0.476。不是稳定可复用元数据；强行复用是近似注意力语义改动。
327	
328	### 3.11 chunked-prefill-size 16384
329	
330	8192 vs 16384：avg `7.605s` vs `7.751s`。chunk 数从 15 减到 8 但单 chunk 退化抵消调度收益（`extend_sparse_fa` 11-12→23-24ms/层，MLP 150→350ms/chunk，GLA 110→205ms）。
331	
332	### 3.12 FP4 tune cache 补 8192 shapes
333	
334	补 MLP/qkv 完整 chunk shape 后 microbench 小幅改善（`gate_up 3.93→3.89ms`，`std_qkv 0.59→0.55ms`，`max_diff=0`），但真实 15chunk wall 在噪声内。不为此更新提交包 cache。
335	
336	### 3.13 FlashInfer sparse stage2 backend / split-KV 参数
337	
338	- `default(auto)+TC` ≈ `7617us`，`fa2+TC` ≈ `7590us`；`fa2`/`fa3`/`cutlass`/`trtllm-gen` 失败或不支持当前 group/shape。
339	- `use_tensor_cores=False` 不支持 `group_size=16`；`fixed_split_size` 更小需 GB 级 workspace；`disable_split_kv` 更慢。
340	- `flash_attn_with_kvcache` sparse page-table 路径不可用：`Can not import FA3 in sgl_kernel`。
341	- 同 `q_len=1` row 化：decode wrapper 与 prefill wrapper `max_diff=0` 且性能相同，强切 prefill wrapper 无收益。
342	
343	stage2 backend 旋钮已枯竭；要继续必须改 KV/block layout 或写等价 kernel。
344	
345	### 3.14 stage1 mask / dtype 快捷路径
346	
347	- `causal=False`：生产 adjusted-q shape 比 `causal=True` 更慢且 topk 大面积变化（`bad 972658/1572864`）。
348	- 输入转 fp16：速度持平，topk 大面积变化（`bad 1500035/1572864`）。
349	- 真实生产 stage1 不是裸 `q=8192`：wrapper 把 32Q/2KV 的 16 group 展到序列维，C++ 的 `max_seqlen_q_adjusted=131072`。早期 `q=8192,1.7ms` microbench 不是线上形态。
350	- 缩 `max_seqlen_q_adjusted` 到 `min(q*16, k1*16)`：变快但 topk 大面积变化（`k1=4095` bad 777303）。adjusted-q 不是单纯 launch bound，参与 causal/pool 边界语义。
351	- `kBlockN=128` stage1 trait（本地构建）：`10.07→16.40ms`，且 `topk_order_same=0.99485`，已撤回。
352	
353	stage1 高收益路必须改 InfLLM C++/CUDA：把 `hdim16_reduce → score → max_pool → topk` 融合直接产出 pooled/topk，或重写完整等价 kernel；不能做 topk 缩限或候选裁剪。
354	
355	### 3.15 GLA chunk kernel 替换
356	
357	| 路径 | 时间 / 一致性 |
358	|---|---|
359	| `chunk_simple_gla` baseline | `0.60ms`，bitwise OK |
360	| `chunk_size=128` | `0.46ms`，**output max_diff=0.25**（不可用）|
361	| `fused_chunk_simple_gla` | `0.83ms`，**更慢且 output 不一致** |
362	
363	GLA 简单 chunk-size/API 替换不可行。要继续必须写形状特化的等价 kernel 或动 qkv layout（v 非 contig stride）。
364	
365	### 3.16 FP4 input quant 复用 / qkv+gate 融合
366	
367	- input quant 复用：命中一致（`fp4_equal=True`），但 wall 在噪声内。
368	- qkv + output gate FP4 GEMM 融合：`mm_fp4` 只有单个 `alpha`，weight `weight_scale_2` 不同（如 L1 lightning q/k/v `0.0001313` vs `z_proj 0.00015394`），强行合并改 bf16 舍入语义。除非有 per-output alpha 的 GEMM。
369	
370	### 3.17 b12x backend
371	
372	`modelopt_quant.py` 当前未接入 b12x dispatch；`b12x_available()` 可 true 但 JIT 编译失败：`cutlass.utils.HardwareInfo().get_max_active_clusters()` 与当前 cutlass DSL MLIR API 不兼容；绕过后在 MLIR object 处 `abort()`（`Expected an MLIR object ... OpResultList`），不是可捕获异常。撤回了 patch。
373	
374	要重启需先修 cutlass DSL/JIT，再做 decode/no-spec 专项 A/B（不是 prefill 主线）。
375	
376	### 3.18 相邻 topk row 合并 / head_group 共享
377	
378	`SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE_TOPK_ADJ=1` profile 真实 line39 第二个 full chunk 起：
379	
380	- 相邻 token `adj_equal`：L0 `0.0414`，其余 sparse layer `0.0001-0.0007`，`mean_run≈1.00, max_run=2`。
381	- 两 head group `head_group_equal`：L0 `0.0013`，其余 `0.0000-0.0010`。
382	
383	不能 exact 合并为 `q_len>1` row，也不能合成共享 page list 的单 wrapper。除非引入近似/per-token mask，方向不可行。
384	
385	### 3.19 stage2 BlockSparse split + LSE merge
386	
387	完整探索（已撤回，备份 `outputs/prefill_experiments/stage2_blocksplit_unlanded.patch`）：
388	
389	- profile 确认第二个 full chunk 起每层 `95 full + 1 partial blocks`。
390	- `BlockSparseAttentionWrapper(R=1,C=64)` 双 head-group forward-only `9.11ms` vs page1 expanded `10.11ms`，离线约 `+10%`；BlockSparse + page64 partial + Triton log2-LSE merge 单 head `4.74→4.95ms`，约 `+4%`，对拍 `cos=0.99999857 max_diff=0.000488`。
391	- 旧 LSE merge 失败原因记录：FlashInfer LSE 是 log2 域，必须用 `2 ** (lse - max_lse)`，不是 `exp`。
392	- gate 唯一可用的是 `metadata.token_pos_in_bs.min() >= topk_tokens`；`extend_prefix_lens_cpu` / `seq_lens_cpu` 都不可靠。
393	- 线上接入 `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE2_BLOCKSPLIT=1`：第二 full chunk 命中 split (`use_split=True, offset=63`)，但 `extend_sparse_fa` 没有下降（118.6ms / 后续 82-84ms），no-profile 15chunk avg `7.114s` vs default `7.110s`。
394	
395	离线 `+4%` forward-only 没穿透 wall，`q_h.contiguous()` / 双 wrapper / merge 开销吃光收益。同构 split 接入路线封死，重启必须换 KV/block layout 或单 wrapper / 单 kernel 的完整等价实现。
396	
397	### 3.20 stage2 page64（双 wrapper）
398	
399	`page_size=64` metadata 从 ~384MB 缩到 ~6MB，离线 page1 vs page64 `diff=0`，plan `46.7→19.5ms`，forward `10.04→8.97ms`。但双 wrapper 破坏 single-wrapper plan-cache 路径，每层 `bf≈18-19ms`。补 `SGLANG_MINICPM_PAGE64_PLAN_CACHE=1` 后 microbench `45.77→9.81ms/call`，但真实 15chunk 与 page1 端到端打平。保留 `SGLANG_MINICPM_STAGE2_BLOCK_PAGE64=1` 实验开关默认 `0`。
400	
401	stage2 真正剩余的算法/layout 路径：单 wrapper KV layout 或 custom block-sparse stage2 kernel。
402	
403	### 3.21 Triton 直写 stage2
404	
405	生产近似 `rows=16384, topk=96x64, seq=131072` bf16：`triton_direct=10.37ms` vs FlashInfer forward + `topk_to_fi_indices=10.13ms`，慢 ~2.4%。tile sweep（`NQ=4096` 单 head-group）最佳 Triton `BH16 BN64 W4 2.69ms` vs FlashInfer `2.27ms`，仍慢 18%。简单 streaming-softmax Triton 不能赢 FlashInfer；要继续需要更接近 FlashInfer 的 split/reduce 结构。
406	
407	### 3.22 stage2 kv dtype 校准
408	
409	`bench/infllmv2/bench_stage2_backends.py` 支持 `STAGE2_KV_DTYPE=bf16|fp8`。当前线上是 bf16 KV，生产形态 `default+TC=10.04ms` 与线上 `fw≈9.8-10.0ms/layer` 对齐。**旧 fp8 口径 ~7.6ms 不能再当 baseline**。
410	
411	### 3.23 MLP dense-as-MoE fused path
412	
413	目标：用 FlashInfer `cutlass_fused_moe` 把 dense MLP 当成 1-expert/top1 MoE，覆盖 `gate_up GEMM -> SiLU*mul FP4 quant -> down GEMM` 整段。
414	
415	验证脚本：`bench/kernels/minicpm/bench_mlp_dense_as_moe_fp4.py`。
416	
417	结果：
418	
419	| Shape | baseline | dense-as-MoE | speedup | diff |
420	|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
421	| M=512 | 0.235ms | 0.322ms | 0.731x | max=0 |
422	| M=2754 | 2.147ms | 2.157ms | 0.995x | max=0 |
423	| M=8192 | 6.119ms | 6.060ms | 1.010x | max=0 |
424	
425	结论：
426	- 数值链条可行：MiniCPM dense `[gate, up]` 需转成 FlashInfer CUTLASS MoE 期望的 `[up, gate]`，scale 同步交换后 synthetic 对拍 `max_diff=0`。
427	- 收益不足：full chunk 仅 `+1.0%/layer`，折到 32 层约 `1.9ms/chunk`，低于接入复杂度和 MoE route/runner 风险。
428	- 工程风险：首次触发会编译 `/user_4813494d/.cache/flashinfer/0.6.8.post1/120f/cached_ops/fused_moe_120/fused_moe_120.so`（约 84MB，目录约 192MB）。默认并发编译曾 exit 137；`MAX_JOBS=1` 可完成但耗时约 80s。
429	
430	不进生产。真正有价值的 MLP 方向仍是 dense FP4 GEMM 自身 epilogue 融合，避免写出 `gate_up` BF16 中间结果，而不是复用 MoE runner。
431	
432	### 3.24 MLP gate_up epilogue 融合调查
433	
434	当前线上 MLP 路径：
435	
436	```text
437	fp4 gate_up GEMM -> BF16 gate_up [M, 2*inter]
438	BF16 gate_up -> silu(gate)*up + FP4 quant
439	FP4 activation -> fp4 down GEMM
440	```
441	
442	profile 证据（15chunk/full chunk 口径）：
443	
444	- `mlp_gate_up_ms≈85-92ms/chunk`
445	- `mlp_swiglu_fp4_quant_ms≈16.2ms/chunk`
446	- `mlp_down_fp4_quantized_ms≈47-51ms/chunk`
447	- gate_up BF16 中间态约 `8192 * 32768 * 2B = 512MB/chunk`，后续 swiglu+quant 再读一遍。
448	
449	核心收益点不是替换 swiglu 小 kernel，而是让 gate_up GEMM epilogue 直接输出 down_proj 需要的 FP4 activation + scale，消掉 BF16 gate_up 的写回/读回和独立 activation launch。
450	
451	已确认的底层边界：
452	
453	- FlashInfer SM120 FP4 GEMM 生产模板当前 epilogue 是普通 `LinearCombination<OutElementType, float, void, float>`，只输出 BF16/FP16。
454	- CUTLASS SM120 已有 `LinCombBlockScaleFactor` / `LinCombEltActBlockScaleFactor` 和 `Sm120BlockScaleFactorRowStore`，支持在 epilogue 生成 FP4 输出和 scale。
455	- 现成 blockscale epilogue是逐元素 unary activation，不能直接表达 SwiGLU 的 pairwise `up * silu(gate)`，也不能把 GEMM 的 `N=2*inter` 存成 `N=inter`。
456	- cuDNN `gemm_swiglu_wrapper_sm100` 和 FlashInfer CuteDSL SM100/103 kernel 语义正确：权重按 `[up32, gate32, ...]` 交错，epilogue 从两个 accumulator subtile 取 up/gate，计算 SwiGLU，可生成 FP4 scale。但当前 RTX 6000D `sm120` 编译失败，报 `expects arch to be one of [Arch.sm_100a, Arch.sm_103a], got Arch.sm_120a`。
457	- TensorRT-LLM/FlashInfer SM120 TMA grouped MoE 代码明确限制：`TMA Warp Specialized Grouped GEMM specialisation doesn't support fused activation`。这解释了 dense-as-MoE 只有约 1% 收益。
458	
459	SM120 blockscale epilogue 探针：
460	
461	脚本：`bench/kernels/minicpm/bench_fp4_blockscale_epilogue_sm120.py`。
462	
463	结果（RTX 6000D，`M=1024,K=4096,N=4096` synthetic）：
464	
465	| 路径 | time |
466	|---|---:|
467	| baseline FP4 GEMM -> BF16 | 0.0935ms |
468	| BF16 post FP4 quant | 0.0203ms |
469	| direct FP4 blockscale epilogue | 0.0620ms |
470	
471	`out_fp4` / `out_sf` 可直接喂现有 `fp4_gemm` down path（`y_sf.view(m, n//16)`）。对比“BF16 落地后再量化”的 down-stream 输出：`max_diff=0.0072, mean_diff=0.000895, ref_abs_mean=0.0260`。这不是 bitwise 等价，但属于可接受的 FP4 量化舍入差异；后续以 e2e 精度/线上验证为准，不再把 bitwise 作为 fused epilogue 的硬目标。
472	
473	e2e 边界：当前 MiniCPM 在线主路径没有“FP4 GEMM 输出 BF16 后立刻 FP4 quant，且中间无非线性”的合法替换点。MLP 是 `gate_up -> SwiGLU -> quant -> down`，direct blockscale epilogue 只能证明“GEMM 直接输出 FP4”底座有收益，不能直接替换 MLP gate_up。真实 e2e 收益必须来自下一步 gated epilogue（accumulator 内直接 `silu(gate)*up` 后输出 FP4）。
474	
475	已否决的中间路线：
476	
477	脚本：`bench/kernels/minicpm/bench_mlp_fp4_intermediate_swiglu.py`（临时脚本已删除，仅保留结论）。
478	
479	尝试把 `gate_up GEMM` 先直接输出 FP4 gate/up，再用 CUDA kernel 做 `dequant gate/up -> SwiGLU -> FP4 quant`，最后接现有 down GEMM。这个路线避免 BF16 gate_up 中间态，但会在非线性前提前 FP4 量化。
480	
481	结果（`M=1024,H=4096,I=4096` synthetic）：
482	
483	| 路径 | time |
484	|---|---:|
485	| baseline gate_up BF16 GEMM | 0.1287ms |
486	| baseline sgl BF16 SwiGLU+FP4 quant | 0.0224ms |
487	| direct gate_up FP4 GEMM | 0.1057ms |
488	| FP4 gate/up SwiGLU+requant kernel | 0.1218ms |
489	
490	路径合计 `0.1511ms -> 0.2274ms`，更慢；down-stream 数值也明显恶化：`mean_diff=1.852 > ref_abs_mean=1.369`，而当前 sgl fused quant 对 BF16 reference 的 `mean_diff=0.0299`。结论：**不能先把 gate/up 中间态压成 FP4 再做 SwiGLU**。性能和精度都不成立。
491	
492	下一步高 ROI 路径：
493	
494	1. 做 SM120 dense-only `gate_up_swiglu_fp4` kernel，而不是继续套 MoE runner。
495	2. 复用 FlashInfer SM120 FP4 GEMM mainloop/tactic，新增 gated blockscale epilogue：
496	   - 输入权重需要从 MiniCPM `[gate, up]` 转为 epilogue友好的 `[up32, gate32, ...]` 或 `[up64, gate64, ...]`。
497	   - epilogue 取成对 accumulator，计算 `silu(gate) * up`。
498	   - 输出 layout 必须直接兼容 `down_proj.forward_fp4_quantized` 需要的 `x_fp4` 和 swizzled/block scale。
499	3. 精度要求：不追求 bitwise；先测 down-stream 数值差异，再做真实请求 / 公开集验证。若 e2e 精度可接受，优先保留 FP32 accumulator direct quantization，因为它才是性能最优路径。
500	
501	## 4. 后续工作流
502	
503	每个新优化必须留下：
504	
505	1. profile 证据（哪里热）。
506	2. 离线一致性（启用 shape + 边界 shape）。
507	3. 在线 debug 一致性（真实请求路径打印命中）。
508	4. smoke：`mars=0`, `max_tokens=200`，输出是人话。
509	5. 15chunk bench：line 39，`max_tokens=1`；不再调用 `/flush_cache`（接口易超时，当前流程按 no-flush 口径记录）。
510	6. profile 复核：热点确实下降，没转成新热点。
511	7. 文档 + commit：可复现命令、关键结果、风险边界。
512	
513	常用启动：
514	
515	```bash
516	EAGLE_MARS_THETA=0 EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA=0 EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA=0 \
517	SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1 SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL=16 \
518	bash eval/start_eagle.sh
519	```
520	
521	停服：
522	
523	```bash
524	bash bench/kill_sglang.sh
525	```
526	
527	用户明确不要 kill 正在用的 server 时必须等待，不做重启验证。
528

862	    def _handle_gpu_memory_settings(self, gpu_mem):
863	        """
864	        Configure GPU memory-dependent settings including
865	        chunked_prefill_size, cuda_graph_max_bs, and mem_fraction_static.
866	
867	        Here are our heuristics:
868	        - Set chunked_prefill_size and cuda_graph_max_bs based on the GPU memory capacity.
869	          This is because GPUs with more memory are generally more powerful, we need to use a larger
870	          chunked_prefill_size and a larger cuda_graph_max_bs to fully utilize the GPU.
871	        - Then set mem_fraction_static based on chunked_prefill_size and cuda_graph_max_bs.
872	
873	          GPU memory capacity = model weights + KV cache pool + activations + cuda graph buffers
874	
875	          The argument mem_fraction_static is defined as (model weights + KV cache pool) / GPU memory capacity,
876	          or equivalently, mem_fraction_static = (GPU memory capacity - activations - cuda graph buffers) / GPU memory capacity.
877	
878	          In order to compute mem_fraction_static, we need to estimate the size of activations and cuda graph buffers.
879	          The activation memory is proportional to the chunked_prefill_size.
880	          The cuda graph memory is proportional to the cuda_graph_max_bs.
881	          We use reserved_mem = chunked_prefill_size * 1.5 + cuda_graph_max_bs * 2 to estimate the size of activations and cuda graph buffers in GB.
882	          and set mem_fraction_static = (GPU memory capacity - reserved_mem) / GPU memory capacity.
883	
884	          The coefficient 1.5 is a heuristic value, in the future, we can do better estimation by looking at the model types, hidden sizes or even do a dummy run.
885	        """
886	        if gpu_mem is not None:
887	            if gpu_mem < 20 * 1024:
888	                # T4, 4080
889	                # (chunked_prefill_size 2k, cuda_graph_max_bs 8)
890	                if self.chunked_prefill_size is None:
891	                    self.chunked_prefill_size = 2048
892	                if self.cuda_graph_max_bs is None:
893	                    self.cuda_graph_max_bs = 8
894	            elif gpu_mem < 35 * 1024:
895	                # A10, 4090, 5090
896	                # (chunked_prefill_size 2k, cuda_graph_max_bs 24 if tp < 4 else 80)
897	                if self.chunked_prefill_size is None:
898	                    self.chunked_prefill_size = 2048
899	                if self.cuda_graph_max_bs is None:
900	                    # Based on detailed statistics, when serving TP1/TP2 models on lower-end GPUs with HBM < 35GB, you can either disable cuda graph or set `cuda_graph_max_bs` to a very small value to reduce the memory overhead of creating cuda graphs, with almost no impact on performance.
901	                    # However, when serving models with TP4 or TP8, we need to enable cuda graph to maintain high performance. In this case, we can set `cuda_graph_max_bs` to 80 (half of the default value 160) to reduce the memory overhead of creating cuda graphs. Looking at the logs
902	                    # from TP4 serving of qwen2-72b, a value of 80 is sufficient and can reduce the memory overhead of creating cuda graphs on lower-end GPUs compared to the original 160, avoiding OOM issues.
903	                    if self.tp_size < 4:
904	                        self.cuda_graph_max_bs = 24
905	                    else:
906	                        self.cuda_graph_max_bs = 80
907	            elif gpu_mem < 60 * 1024:
908	                # A100 (40GB), L40,
909	                # (chunked_prefill_size 4k, cuda_graph_max_bs 32 if tp < 4 else 160)
910	                if self.chunked_prefill_size is None:
911	                    self.chunked_prefill_size = 4096
912	                if self.cuda_graph_max_bs is None:
913	                    if self.tp_size < 4:
914	                        self.cuda_graph_max_bs = 32
915	                    else:
916	                        self.cuda_graph_max_bs = 160
917	            elif gpu_mem < 90 * 1024:
918	                # H100, A100

920	                if self.chunked_prefill_size is None:
921	                    self.chunked_prefill_size = 8192

20	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

22	
23	@dataclasses.dataclass
24	class ConcreteSizeEntry:
25	    runtime_shape: int
26	    need_to_compile: bool  # the size is in compile_sizes
27	    use_cudagraph: bool  # the size is in cudagraph_capture_sizes
28	
29	    compiled: bool = False
30	    runnable: Callable = None  # type: ignore
31	    num_finished_warmup: int = 0
32	    cudagraph: Optional[torch.cuda.CUDAGraph] = None
33	    output: Optional[Any] = None
34	
35	    # for cudagraph debugging, track the input addresses
36	    # during capture, and check if they are the same during replay
37	    input_addresses: Optional[list[int]] = None
38	
39	
40	class CUDAPiecewiseBackend:
41	
42	    def __init__(
43	        self,
44	        graph: fx.GraphModule,
45	        compile_config: CompilationConfig,
46	        inductor_config: dict[str, Any],
47	        graph_pool: Any,
48	        piecewise_compile_index: int,
49	        total_piecewise_compiles: int,
50	        sym_shape_indices: list[int],
51	        compiled_graph_for_general_shape: Callable,
52	        sglang_backend,
53	    ):
54	        """
55	        The backend for piecewise compilation.
56	        It mainly handles the compilation and cudagraph capturing.
57	
58	        We will compile `self.graph` once for the general shape,
59	        and then compile for different shapes specified in
60	        `compilation_config.compile_sizes`.
61	
62	        Independently, we will capture cudagraph for different shapes.
63	
64	        If a shape needs both compilation and cudagraph, we will
65	        compile it first, and then capture cudagraph.
66	        """
67	        self.graph = graph
68	        self.inductor_config = inductor_config
69	        self.graph_pool = graph_pool
70	        self.piecewise_compile_index = piecewise_compile_index
71	        self.total_piecewise_compiles = total_piecewise_compiles
72	        self.sglang_backend = sglang_backend
73	
74	        self.is_first_graph = piecewise_compile_index == 0
75	        self.is_last_graph = piecewise_compile_index == total_piecewise_compiles - 1
76	
77	        self.compile_sizes: set[int] = set([])
78	        self.compile_config = compile_config
79	        self.cudagraph_capture_sizes: set[int] = set(compile_config.get_capture_sizes())
80	
81	        self.first_run_finished = False
82	
83	        self.compiled_graph_for_general_shape = compiled_graph_for_general_shape  # noqa
84	
85	        self.sym_shape_indices = sym_shape_indices
86	
87	        # the entries for different shapes that we need to either
88	        # compile or capture cudagraph
89	        self.concrete_size_entries: dict[int, ConcreteSizeEntry] = {}
90	
91	        # to_be_compiled_sizes tracks the remaining sizes to compile,
92	        # and updates during the compilation process, so we need to copy it
93	        self.to_be_compiled_sizes: set[int] = self.compile_sizes.copy()
94	        for shape in self.compile_sizes.union(self.cudagraph_capture_sizes):
95	            self.concrete_size_entries[shape] = ConcreteSizeEntry(
96	                runtime_shape=shape,
97	                need_to_compile=shape in self.compile_sizes,
98	                use_cudagraph=shape in self.cudagraph_capture_sizes,
99	            )
100	
101	    def check_for_ending_compilation(self):
102	        if self.is_last_graph and not self.to_be_compiled_sizes:
103	            # no specific sizes to compile
104	            # save the hash of the inductor graph for the next run
105	            self.sglang_backend.compiler_manager.save_to_file()
106	
107	    def __call__(self, *args) -> Any:
108	        if not self.first_run_finished:
109	            self.first_run_finished = True
110	            self.check_for_ending_compilation()
111	            return self.compiled_graph_for_general_shape(*args)
112	
113	        if len(self.sym_shape_indices) == 0:
114	            return self.compiled_graph_for_general_shape(*args)
115	
116	        runtime_shape = args[self.sym_shape_indices[0]]
117	        if runtime_shape not in self.concrete_size_entries:
118	            # we don't need to do anything for this shape
119	            return self.compiled_graph_for_general_shape(*args)
120	
121	        entry = self.concrete_size_entries[runtime_shape]
122	
123	        if entry.runnable is None:
124	            entry.runnable = self.compiled_graph_for_general_shape
125	
126	        if entry.need_to_compile and not entry.compiled:
127	            entry.compiled = True
128	            self.to_be_compiled_sizes.remove(runtime_shape)
129	            # args are real arguments
130	            entry.runnable = self.sglang_backend.compiler_manager.compile(
131	                self.graph,
132	                args,
133	                self.inductor_config,
134	                graph_index=self.piecewise_compile_index,
135	                num_graphs=self.total_piecewise_compiles,
136	                runtime_shape=runtime_shape,
137	            )
138	
139	            # finished compilations for all required shapes
140	            if self.is_last_graph and not self.to_be_compiled_sizes:
141	                self.check_for_ending_compilation()
142	
143	        # Skip CUDA graphs if this entry doesn't use them OR
144	        # if we're supposed to skip them globally
145	        # skip_cuda_graphs = get_forward_context().skip_cuda_graphs
146	        # if not entry.use_cudagraph or skip_cuda_graphs:
147	        #     return entry.runnable(*args)
148	        if is_in_pcg_torch_compile():
149	            return entry.runnable(*args)
150	
151	        if entry.cudagraph is None:
152	            if entry.num_finished_warmup < 1:  # noqa
153	                entry.num_finished_warmup += 1
154	                return entry.runnable(*args)
155	
156	            if self.compile_config.get_enable_debug_mode():
157	                input_addresses = [
158	                    x.data_ptr() for x in args if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor)
159	                ]
160	                entry.input_addresses = input_addresses
161	            cudagraph = torch.cuda.CUDAGraph()
162	
163	            with ExitStack() as stack:
164	                if not self.is_first_graph:
165	                    # during every model forward, we will capture
166	                    # many pieces of cudagraphs (roughly one per layer).
167	                    # running gc again and again across layers will
168	                    # make the cudagraph capture very slow.
169	                    # therefore, we only run gc for the first graph,
170	                    # and disable gc for the rest of the graphs.
171	                    stack.enter_context(patch("gc.collect", lambda: None))
172	                    stack.enter_context(patch("torch.cuda.empty_cache", lambda: None))
173	                # mind-exploding: carefully manage the reference and memory.
174	                stream = get_pcg_capture_stream()
175	                assert stream is not None, "PCG capture stream is not set"
176	                with torch.cuda.graph(cudagraph, pool=self.graph_pool, stream=stream):
177	                    # `output` is managed by pytorch's cudagraph pool
178	                    output = entry.runnable(*args)
179	                    if self.is_last_graph:
180	                        # by converting it to weak ref,
181	                        # the original `output` will immediately be released
182	                        # to save memory. It is only safe to do this for
183	                        # the last graph, because the output of the last graph
184	                        # will not be used by any other cuda graph.
185	                        output = weak_ref_tensors(output)
186	
187	            # here we always use weak ref for the output
188	            # to save memory
189	            entry.output = weak_ref_tensors(output)
190	            entry.cudagraph = cudagraph
191	
192	            compilation_counter.num_cudagraph_captured += 1
193	
194	            # important: we need to return the output, rather than
195	            # the weak ref of the output, so that pytorch can correctly
196	            # manage the memory during cuda graph capture
197	            return output
198	
199	        if self.compile_config.get_enable_debug_mode():
200	            # check if the input addresses are the same
201	            new_input_addresses = [
202	                x.data_ptr() for x in args if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor)
203	            ]
204	            assert new_input_addresses == entry.input_addresses, (
205	                "Input addresses for cudagraphs are different during replay."
206	                f" Expected {entry.input_addresses}, got {new_input_addresses}"
207	            )
208	        entry.cudagraph.replay()
209	        return entry.output
210

75

78	    ):
79	        self.disable_cache = disable_cache
80	        self.cache_dir = cache_dir
81	        self.cache_file_path = os.path.join(cache_dir, "sglang_compile_cache.py")
82	
83	        if not disable_cache and os.path.exists(self.cache_file_path):
84	            with open(self.cache_file_path) as f:
85	                self.cache = ast.literal_eval(f.read())
86	
87	        self.compiler.initialize_cache(
88	            cache_dir=cache_dir, disable_cache=disable_cache, prefix=prefix
89	        )
90	
91	    def save_to_file(self):
92	        if self.disable_cache or not self.is_cache_updated:
93	            return

95	        data = printer.pformat(self.cache)
96	        with open(self.cache_file_path, "w") as f:
97	            f.write(data)

66	@contextmanager
67	def freeze_gc(enable_cudagraph_gc: bool):
68	    """
69	    Optimize garbage collection during CUDA graph capture.
70	    Clean up, then freeze all remaining objects from being included
71	    in future collections if GC is disabled during capture.
72	    """
73	    gc.collect()
74	    should_freeze = not enable_cudagraph_gc
75	    if should_freeze:
76	        gc.freeze()
77	    try:
78	        yield
79	    finally:
80	        if should_freeze:
81	            gc.unfreeze()
82	
83	
84	def _to_torch(model: torch.nn.Module, reverse: bool, num_tokens: int):
85	    for sub in model._modules.values():
86	        if isinstance(sub, MultiPlatformOp):
87	            if reverse:
88	                sub.leave_torch_compile()
89	            else:
90	                sub.enter_torch_compile(num_tokens=num_tokens)
91	        if isinstance(sub, torch.nn.Module):
92	            _to_torch(sub, reverse, num_tokens)
93	
94	
95	@contextmanager
96	def patch_model(model: torch.nn.Module, compiler: str):
97	    try:
98	        if compiler != "eager":
99	            _to_torch(model, reverse=False, num_tokens=16)
100	        yield model
101	    finally:
102	        _to_torch(model, reverse=True, num_tokens=16)

115	    global_graph_memory_pool = val

123	    if hasattr(torch._dynamo.config, "cache_size_limit"):
124	        torch._dynamo.config.cache_size_limit = 1024

127	class PiecewiseCudaGraphRunner:

192	            self.input_ids = torch.zeros((self.max_num_tokens,), dtype=torch.int64)
193	            self.out_cache_loc = torch.zeros(

195	            )

198	                if model_runner.is_hybrid_swa

200	            )

205	            )
206	            self.mamba_track_mask = (

211	            self.mamba_track_seqlens = (
212	                torch.zeros((self.max_bs,), dtype=torch.int32)
213	                if self.mamba_track_enabled

215	            )
216	            self.positions = torch.zeros((self.max_num_tokens,), dtype=torch.int64)

218	            self.tbo_plugin = TboCudaGraphRunnerPlugin()
219	
220	            if (
221	                self.is_multimodal
222	            ):  # Only create input_embeds and mrope_positions for multimodal model to save memory
223	                # 1. In multimodal, we only compile and capture the language model part.
224	                # 2. The embedder is outside of the graph, but cuda graph requires the input embeds to have a fixed memory address.

239	        # Set graph pool id globally to be able to use symmetric memory
240	        set_graph_pool_id(get_global_graph_memory_pool())
241	
242	        with enable_piecewise_cuda_graph():
243	            with patch_model(

245	            ) as patched_model:
246	                install_torch_compiled(
247	                    patched_model,
248	                    fullgraph=True,
249	                    dynamic_arg_dims=None,
250	                    compile_config=self.compile_config,

252	                )

254	                with set_compiled(True), enable_piecewise_cuda_graph_compile():
255	                    compile_range = (
256	                        tqdm.tqdm(list(reversed(self.capture_num_tokens)))
257	                        if get_tensor_model_parallel_rank() == 0

259	                    )
260	                    for _, num_tokens in enumerate(compile_range):

266	
267	                set_global_graph_memory_pool(self.device_module.graph_pool_handle())
268	                set_graph_pool_id(get_global_graph_memory_pool())
269	
270	                self.device_module.synchronize()
271	                self.model_runner.tp_group.barrier()
272	                # Capture
273	                try:
274	                    self.capture()
275	                except RuntimeError as e:
276	                    raise Exception(
277	                        f"Capture cuda graph failed: {e}\n{PIECEWISE_CUDA_GRAPH_CAPTURE_FAILED_MSG}"
278	                    )
279	
280	        self.raw_num_tokens = 0

369	    def can_run(self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch):
370	        num_tokens = len(forward_batch.input_ids)
371	        if forward_batch.return_logprob:
372	            for start_len, seq_len in zip(
373	                forward_batch.extend_logprob_start_lens_cpu,
374	                forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu,
375	            ):

377	                    return False
378	        if num_tokens <= self.max_num_tokens:
379	            return True
380	        return False

382	    def capture(self) -> None:
383	        # Trigger CUDA graph capture for specific shapes.
384	        # Capture the large shapes first so that the smaller shapes
385	        # can reuse the memory pool allocated for the large shapes.
386	        with freeze_gc(

388	        ), graph_capture() as graph_capture_context:
389	            stream = graph_capture_context.stream
390	            with set_pcg_capture_stream(stream):
391	                avail_mem = get_available_gpu_memory(
392	                    self.model_runner.device,
393	                    self.model_runner.gpu_id,
394	                    empty_cache=False,
395	                )
396	                # Reverse the order to enable better memory sharing across cuda graphs.
397	                capture_range = (
398	                    tqdm.tqdm(list(reversed(self.capture_num_tokens)))
399	                    if get_tensor_model_parallel_rank() == 0
400	                    else reversed(self.capture_num_tokens)
401	                )
402	                for i, num_tokens in enumerate(capture_range):
403	                    if get_tensor_model_parallel_rank() == 0:

407	                            empty_cache=False,
408	                        )
409	                        capture_range.set_description(
410	                            f"Capturing num tokens ({num_tokens=} {avail_mem=:.2f} GB)"
411	                        )
412	
413	                    with set_compiled(True):
414	                        self.capture_one_batch_size(num_tokens)

508	            forward_batch.dp_local_start_pos = forward_batch.dp_local_num_tokens = None
509	            set_dp_buffer_len(

539	    def replay_prepare(
540	        self,
541	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
542	        **kwargs,
543	    ):
544	        num_tokens = len(forward_batch.input_ids)
545	        index = bisect.bisect_left(self.capture_num_tokens, num_tokens)
546	        static_num_tokens = self.capture_num_tokens[index]
547	        self.raw_num_tokens = num_tokens
548	        if static_num_tokens != num_tokens:
549	            self.out_cache_loc.zero_()
550	            if self.out_cache_loc_swa is not None:
551	                self.out_cache_loc_swa.zero_()
552	        bs = forward_batch.batch_size
553	
554	        self.input_ids[:num_tokens].copy_(forward_batch.input_ids)
555	        self.positions[:num_tokens].copy_(forward_batch.positions)
556	        self.out_cache_loc[:num_tokens].copy_(forward_batch.out_cache_loc)
557	        if self.out_cache_loc_swa is not None:
558	            self.out_cache_loc_swa[: self.raw_num_tokens].copy_(
559	                self.model_runner.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.translate_loc_from_full_to_swa(
560	                    forward_batch.out_cache_loc
561	                )
562	            )
563	
564	        if (
565	            self.mamba_track_indices is not None
566	            and forward_batch.mamba_track_indices is not None
567	        ):
568	            self.mamba_track_indices[:bs].copy_(forward_batch.mamba_track_indices)
569	        if (
570	            self.mamba_track_mask is not None
571	            and forward_batch.mamba_track_mask is not None
572	        ):
573	            self.mamba_track_mask[:bs].copy_(forward_batch.mamba_track_mask)
574	        if (
575	            self.mamba_track_seqlens is not None
576	            and forward_batch.mamba_track_seqlens is not None
577	        ):
578	            self.mamba_track_seqlens[:bs].copy_(forward_batch.mamba_track_seqlens)

139	@register_custom_op(mutates_args=["output"])
140	def unified_attention_with_output(
141	    query: torch.Tensor,
142	    key: torch.Tensor,
143	    value: torch.Tensor,
144	    output: torch.Tensor,
145	    save_kv_cache: bool,
146	    layer_id: int,
147	    *,
148	    q_rope: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
149	    k_rope: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
150	    sinks: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
151	) -> None:
152	    context = get_forward_context()
153	    forward_batch = context.forward_batch
154	    attention_layers = context.attention_layers
155	    attention_layer = attention_layers[layer_id]
156	
157	    kwargs = {}
158	    if q_rope is not None:
159	        kwargs["q_rope"] = q_rope
160	    if k_rope is not None:
161	        kwargs["k_rope"] = k_rope
162	    if sinks is not None:
163	        kwargs["sinks"] = sinks
164	
165	    ret = forward_batch.attn_backend.forward(
166	        query, key, value, attention_layer, forward_batch, save_kv_cache, **kwargs
167	    )
168	    assert (
169	        output.numel() == ret.numel()
170	    ), f"Output tensor element mismatch: {output.numel()} != {ret.numel()}"
171	
172	    output.view(ret.shape).copy_(ret)
173	    return

> AGENT

完整 grep + 看代码后给结论。

## 我们是否启用了 piecewise prefill

**没有**。两个区分：

| 概念 | 含义 | 我们 |
|---|---|---|
| **Chunked prefill** | 按 token 数把长 prefill 拆 chunk（scheduler 层） | ✅ `--chunked-prefill-size 8192`，15 chunks / 117K |
| **Piecewise CUDA graph (prefill)** | 把单 chunk 内 forward graph 在 attention/all-reduce 切段，非 attn 段 capture CUDA graph 消 launch overhead（compilation 层） | ❌ 默认 `enable_piecewise_cuda_graph=False`，`SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 没开 |

你大概率问的是后者。SGLang 上游叫 `--enable-piecewise-cuda-graph`，代码在 `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/compilation/{backend,cuda_piecewise_backend,piecewise_context_manager}.py` + `model_executor/piecewise_cuda_graph_runner.py`。原理：在 `unified_attention_with_output`/`gdn_with_output`/`inplace_all_reduce` 三个 split op 处切 fx graph，每个非-attn 子图按 `num_tokens` 桶（4..8192，约 90 个桶）capture CUDA graph，attention 走 eager 保留动态 shape + metadata。

## 启用难点

**Hard blockers**：

1. **GLA 路径没 split 边界** — `minicpm.py:1172` 直接调 `linear_attn_backend.forward(...)`，**不走** `RadixAttention.unified_attention_with_output`。默认 split_ops 只切 `gdn_with_output`（qwen3-next 用），不切 simple-GLA。我们 24/32 层 GLA 会被强行塞进 CUDA graph，但 `chunk_simple_gla` 是 triton kernel + 内部动态形状，capture 时大概率崩。**必须** 给 GLA 注册一个新的 `register_custom_op` split 点，写法仿 `unified_attention_with_output`。

2. **profile hook 全员散布** — 主路径几十处 `_evt_record/_evt_end` 在 CUDA graph 内不允许（cudaEvent create/record 改 graph 状态）。要么全删，要么 `if not is_in_piecewise_cuda_graph()` guard 一遍。

3. **FlashInfer plan-cache 的 buffer 语义** — §2.1 跨 forward 复用 `wrapper._paged_kv_indptr_buf` + 每次刷新内容。CUDA graph replay 必须 fixed-address，我们已经是 in-place 写持久 buffer，**这点是契合的**，但需要确保 plan cache 在 capture 后绝对不 reallocate。一旦 plan 失效重 allocate，崩。

4. **§2.10 direct topk → FI indices 的 persistent empty buffer** — 已经是 fixed-address，正好契合；但 `kv_indptr/indices/lpl` 的有效区由 `kv_indptr` 限定，capture 后 replay 时如果 valid range 变大（实际 topk × seq 更长）会越界。需要在 capture warmup 阶段就跑最大 case。

5. **chunk 桶 vs 实际形状** — 我们 stable 形态是 `M=8192`，但首/尾 chunk 形状不齐（profile 显示首 chunk 与 full chunk 行为不同：plan miss、stage1 actual maxlen 切不掉、`max_pooling_1d_varlen` full-layout fallback）。这些 case 多数会落到 piecewise 桶的 padding 上、或直接 fallback eager，复杂度高。

6. **mem-fraction 紧** — 我们 `0.75`，预算紧；90 个 capture 桶占用每桶预留 activation buffer。`server_args.py:1004` 显示 piecewise 启用会再加 `chunked_prefill_size // 8 = 1024 MB` 预留。挤压 KV cache pool，长上下文 prefill 直接吃亏。

7. **EAGLE-3 spec verify 路径** — verify 是 EXTEND-like (`adjusted_q ×16`)，piecewise capture mode 是 EXTEND，名义上覆盖；但 spec_info / hidden_states 的传递与 chain verify 树拓扑，需要逐项跟 SGLang 上游验过的模型对，我们没现成 hybrid + GLA + EAGLE-3 + InfLLM-v2 sparse + piecewise 同时启用的参考实现。

**Soft（不算 blocker）**：

- TP=1 → 不触发 `inplace_all_reduce` split op
- `--disable-radix-cache` 已开 → 减少 prefix 路径分支
- 非多模态 → 无 embed buffer 问题

## 收益估算

我们 PROFILE=1 单 chunk wall ≈ 460-490ms，几乎全是 GPU compute/memory bound（MLP 133 + GLA 120 + sparse_fa 84 + stage1 74 + 其他 ≈ 单 chunk wall）。

粗算 CPU launch overhead：
- GLA layer ~8-10 launch/layer × 24 = 200 launches
- Standard layer ~12-15 launch/layer × 8 = 100 launches  
- 单 chunk ~300 launches × 5us ≈ **1.5ms CPU overhead / chunk**
- 15 chunks: **~22ms / 7s ≈ 0.3% 理论上限**

实际可能高一点（含 metadata sync），但封顶估计 **wall 1-2%**。

对比成本：
- 工程量：注册 GLA split op + 清 profile hook + 复核 fused kernel buffer + 复核 FlashInfer plan-cache replay 行为 + 跑全量精度回归 + 显存调参 → **2-3 周**
- 风险：碰到 hybrid + sparse + spec 三路径联调的全新组合，对上游零先例
- 副作用：90 桶预编译 + capture，启动多 60-90s；显存预留挤压 KV pool

## 结论

**不建议启用**。我们的 prefill **已经不是 launch-bound**（§2.12-2.15 的算子融合已经把 launch 数压得很低，wall ≈ kernel sum）。Piecewise 的 ROI 在我们这种场景已经被前面的 fused kernel 工作吃掉了大半。

**真要做先验真**：
1. 跑一次 PyTorch profiler 看 `cuda_time / wall_time` 比例（用 `bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` line 39 单 chunk）。若 GPU 利用率 > 95% → 直接放弃。
2. 若 idle 显著（>5%），先定位最大 idle gap 来源（多半是某个 H2D sync 或 metadata 计算），单点解就行，不必动整套 piecewise。

人话：方向对、上游已有、但对我们 ROI 低 + 工程量大 + 风险高。把同样的工程预算投到 §2.11 stage1 算法级改动或 MLP `gate_up_swiglu_fp4` epilogue 融合上，收益预期高一个数量级。

205	@dataclasses.dataclass
206	class SplitItem:
207	    submod_name: str
208	    graph_id: int
209	    is_splitting_graph: bool
210	    graph: fx.GraphModule
211	
212	
213	def split_graph(
214	    graph: fx.GraphModule, ops: list[str]
215	) -> tuple[fx.GraphModule, list[SplitItem]]:
216	    # split graph by ops
217	    subgraph_id = 0
218	    node_to_subgraph_id = {}
219	    split_op_graphs = []
220	    for node in graph.graph.nodes:
221	        if node.op in ("output", "placeholder"):
222	            continue
223	        if node.op == "call_function" and str(node.target) in ops:
224	            subgraph_id += 1
225	            node_to_subgraph_id[node] = subgraph_id
226	            split_op_graphs.append(subgraph_id)
227	            subgraph_id += 1
228	        else:
229	            node_to_subgraph_id[node] = subgraph_id
230	
231	    # `keep_original_order` is important!
232	    # otherwise pytorch might reorder the nodes and
233	    # the semantics of the graph will change when we
234	    # have mutations in the graph
235	    split_gm = torch.fx.passes.split_module.split_module(
236	        graph, None, lambda node: node_to_subgraph_id[node], keep_original_order=True
237	    )
238	
239	    outputs = []
240	
241	    names = [name for (name, module) in split_gm.named_modules()]
242	
243	    for name in names:
244	        if "." in name or name == "":
245	            # recursive child module or the user_4813494d module
246	            continue
247	
248	        module = getattr(split_gm, name)
249	
250	        graph_id = int(name.replace("submod_", ""))
251	        outputs.append(SplitItem(name, graph_id, (graph_id in split_op_graphs), module))
252	
253	    # sort by intetger graph_id, rather than string name
254	    outputs.sort(key=lambda x: x.graph_id)
255	
256	    return split_gm, outputs
257

259	# we share the global graph pool among all the backends
260	global_graph_pool = None

265	class PiecewiseCompileInterpreter(torch.fx.Interpreter):
266	    def __init__(
267	        self,
268	        module: torch.fx.GraphModule,
269	        compile_submod_names: list[str],
270	        inductor_config: dict[str, Any],
271	        graph_pool,
272	        compile_config: CompilationConfig,
273	        sglang_backend: "SGLangBackend",
274	    ):
275	        super().__init__(module)
276	        from torch._guards import detect_fake_mode
277	
278	        self.fake_mode = detect_fake_mode()
279	        self.compile_submod_names = compile_submod_names
280	        self.graph_pool = graph_pool
281	        self.sglang_backend = sglang_backend
282	        # When True, it annoyingly dumps the torch.fx.Graph on errors.
283	        self.extra_traceback = False
284	        self.inductor_config = inductor_config
285	        self.compile_config = compile_config
286	
287	    def run(self, *args):
288	        fake_args = [
289	            self.fake_mode.from_tensor(t) if isinstance(t, torch.Tensor) else t
290	            for t in args
291	        ]
292	        with self.fake_mode, enable_python_dispatcher():
293	            return super().run(*fake_args)
294	
295	    def call_module(
296	        self,
297	        target: torch.fx.node.Target,
298	        args: tuple[torch.fx.node.Argument, ...],
299	        kwargs: dict[str, Any],
300	    ) -> Any:
301	        assert isinstance(target, str)
302	        output = super().call_module(target, args, kwargs)
303	
304	        if target in self.compile_submod_names:
305	            index = self.compile_submod_names.index(target)
306	            submod = self.fetch_attr(target)
307	            sym_shape_indices = [
308	                i for i, x in enumerate(args) if isinstance(x, torch.SymInt)
309	            ]
310	            global compilation_start_time
311	            compiled_graph_for_dynamic_shape = (
312	                self.sglang_backend.compiler_manager.compile(
313	                    submod,
314	                    args,
315	                    self.inductor_config,
316	                    graph_index=index,
317	                    num_graphs=len(self.compile_submod_names),
318	                    runtime_shape=None,
319	                )
320	            )
321	
322	            self.module.__dict__[target] = make_backend(
323	                submod,
324	                self.compile_config,
325	                self.inductor_config,
326	                self.graph_pool,
327	                index,
328	                len(self.compile_submod_names),
329	                sym_shape_indices,
330	                compiled_graph_for_dynamic_shape,
331	                self.sglang_backend,
332	            )
333	
334	            compilation_counter.num_piecewise_capturable_graphs_seen += 1
335	
336	        return output

358

378	        rank0_log(f"Initializing SGLangBackend")

382	        self.post_grad_pass_manager = PostGradPassManager()
383	        self.sym_tensor_indices = []
384	        self.input_buffers = []

386	        self.compiler_manager = CompilerManager(config)
387	        self.inductor_config = {
388	            "enable_auto_functionalized_v2": False,
389	        }
390	        self.compile_config = config
391	
392	    def configure_post_pass(self):
393	        self.post_grad_pass_manager.configure()
394	        self.inductor_config["post_grad_custom_post_pass"] = self.post_grad_pass_manager
395

398	        base_cache_dir = os.path.expanduser(
399	            os.getenv("SGLANG_CACHE_DIR", "~/.cache/sglang/")

396	    def __call__(self, graph: fx.GraphModule, example_inputs) -> Callable:
397	        rank0_log(f"SGLangBackend __call__")
398	        base_cache_dir = os.path.expanduser(
399	            os.getenv("SGLANG_CACHE_DIR", "~/.cache/sglang/")
400	        )
401	
402	        cache_hash = self.compiler_manager.compute_hash()
403	        cache_dir = os.path.join(
404	            base_cache_dir,
405	            "torch_compile_cache",
406	            cache_hash,
407	        )
408	
409	        os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
410	        rank = 0
411	        dp_rank = 0
412	        local_cache_dir = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"rank_{rank}_{dp_rank}", model_tag)
413	        os.makedirs(local_cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
414	        self.compiler_manager.initialize_cache(
415	            local_cache_dir, disable_cache=False, prefix=""
416	        )
417	        compilation_counter.num_graphs_seen += 1
418	
419	        assert not self._called, "SGLangBackend can only be called once"
420	
421	        self.graph = graph
422	        self.configure_post_pass()
423	
424	        self.split_gm, self.piecewise_graphs = split_graph(
425	            graph,
426	            self.compile_config.split_ops,
427	        )

429

431	        # for debugging, no need to print the graph here
432	        lazy_format_graph_code("before split", self.graph)
433	        lazy_format_graph_code("after split", self.split_gm)
434	
435	        compilation_counter.num_piecewise_graphs_seen += len(self.piecewise_graphs)
436	
437	        submod_names_to_compile = [

449	            self,
450	        ).run(*example_inputs)

455	            graph_path = os.path.join(
456	                local_cache_dir, f"computation_graph_{time.time()}.py"
457	            )
458	            if not os.path.exists(graph_path):
459	                # code adapted from https://github.com/thuml/depyf/blob/dab831108a752d1facc00acdd6d4243891845c37/depyf/explain/patched_lazy_format_graph_code.py#L30 # noqa

30	@dataclass
31	class IntermediateTensors:
32	    """For all pipeline stages except the last, we need to return the hidden
33	    states and residuals to be sent to the next stage. This data structure
34	    contains the hidden states and residuals for a request.
35	
36	    Each stage also needs to handle its own finished_sending and
37	    finished_recving in case of kv transfer.
38	    """
39	
40	    tensors: dict[str, torch.Tensor]
41	    # [req_ids]
42	    finished_sending: Optional[set[str]] = None
43	    finished_recving: Optional[set[str]] = None
44	
45	    def __init__(self, tensors):
46	        # manually define this function, so that
47	        # Dynamo knows `IntermediateTensors()` comes from this file.
48	        # Otherwise, dataclass will generate this function by evaluating
49	        # a string, and we will lose the information about the source file.
50	        self.tensors = tensors
51	
52	    def __getitem__(self, key: Union[str, slice]):
53	        if isinstance(key, str):
54	            return self.tensors[key]
55	        elif isinstance(key, slice):
56	            return self.__class__({k: v[key] for k, v in self.tensors.items()})
57	
58	    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: torch.Tensor):
59	        self.tensors[key] = value
60	
61	    def items(self):
62	        return self.tensors.items()
63	
64	    def __len__(self):
65	        return len(self.tensors)
66	
67	    def __eq__(self, other: object):
68	        return isinstance(other, self.__class__) and self
69	
70	    def __repr__(self) -> str:
71	        return f"IntermediateTensors(tensors={self.tensors})"
72	
73	
74	def _normalize_dims(dims, ndim: int):
75	    dims = [dims] if isinstance(dims, int) else list(dims)
76	    return [d if d >= 0 else ndim + d for d in dims]
77	
78	
79	class _MaybeIntermediateTensors:
80	    """Duck-typed check to support your IntermediateTensors without importing."""
81	
82	    def __init__(self, obj):
83	        self.is_intermediate = hasattr(obj, "tensors") and isinstance(
84	            getattr(obj, "tensors"), dict
85	        )
86	        self.obj = obj
87	
88	
89	def _mark_dynamic_on_value(val, dims):
90	    if isinstance(val, torch.Tensor):
91	        torch._dynamo.mark_dynamic(val, _normalize_dims(dims, val.ndim))
92	    else:
93	        mit = _MaybeIntermediateTensors(val)
94	        if mit.is_intermediate:
95	            for t in mit.obj.tensors.values():
96	                torch._dynamo.mark_dynamic(t, _normalize_dims(dims, t.ndim))
97	        # else: ignore (None or non-tensor)
98	
99	
100	def _infer_dynamic_arg_dims_from_annotations(forward_fn):
101	    sig = inspect.signature(forward_fn)
102	    dyn = {}
103	    for name, p in sig.parameters.items():
104	        ann = p.annotation
105	        # Accept torch.Tensor / Optional[torch.Tensor] / your IntermediateTensors types by name
106	        if (
107	            ann is torch.Tensor
108	            or getattr(getattr(ann, "__args__", [None])[0], "__name__", "") == "Tensor"
109	        ):
110	            dyn[name] = 0
111	        elif getattr(ann, "__name__", "") in ("IntermediateTensors",) or any(
112	            getattr(a, "__name__", "") == "IntermediateTensors"
113	            for a in getattr(ann, "__args__", [])
114	        ):
115	            dyn[name] = 0
116	        elif ann == "torch.Tensor" or ann == "Optional[torch.Tensor]":
117	            # For future import annotations (e.g. from __future__ import annotations), the annotation is a string
118	            dyn[name] = 0
119	    if not dyn:
120	        raise ValueError("No dynamic dims inferred; pass dynamic_arg_dims explicitly.")
121	    return dyn
122	
123	
124	def install_torch_compiled(
125	    module: torch.nn.Module,
126	    *,
127	    dynamic_arg_dims: dict[str, Union[int, list[int]]] | None = None,
128	    backend_factory: Optional[Callable[[torch.fx.GraphModule, list], Callable]] = None,
129	    compile_config: CompilationConfig = None,
130	    fullgraph: bool = True,
131	    graph_pool: Any = None,
132	):
133	    rank0_log(f"install_torch_compiled")
134	    unbound_fwd = module.__class__.forward
135	    if not callable(unbound_fwd):
136	        raise TypeError("module.__class__.forward must be callable")
137	    original_code = unbound_fwd.__code__
138	
139	    dyn_map = dynamic_arg_dims or _infer_dynamic_arg_dims_from_annotations(unbound_fwd)
140	
141	    if backend_factory is None:
142	        from sglang.srt.compilation.backend import SGLangBackend
143	
144	        backend_factory = lambda gm, ex: SGLangBackend(compile_config, graph_pool)(
145	            gm, ex
146	        )
147	
148	    compiled_codes: list[type(original_code)] = []
149	    state = {"compiled": False, "compiled_callable": None}
150	
151	    def bytecode_hook(old_code, new_code):
152	        if old_code is not original_code:
153	            return
154	        frame = sys._getframe()
155	        while frame and frame.f_back:
156	            frame = frame.f_back
157	            if (
158	                frame.f_code.co_name == "_compile"
159	                and os.path.basename(frame.f_code.co_filename) == "convert_frame.py"
160	            ):
161	                break
162	        try:
163	            dynamo_frame = frame.f_locals["frame"]
164	        except Exception:
165	            return
166	        if dynamo_frame.f_code is not old_code:
167	            return
168	        if dynamo_frame.f_locals.get("self") is not module:
169	            return
170	        compiled_codes.append(new_code)
171	
172	    torch._dynamo.convert_frame.register_bytecode_hook(bytecode_hook)
173	
174	    def _ensure_compiled(self, *args, **kwargs):
175	        """Compile on first use (with flag ON)."""
176	        if state["compiled"]:
177	            return
178	        # Mark dynamic dims only when we are about to compile
179	        sig = inspect.signature(unbound_fwd)
180	        ba = sig.bind(self, *args, **kwargs)
181	        ba.apply_defaults()
182	        for name, dims in (dyn_map or {}).items():
183	            if name in ba.arguments:
184	                val = ba.arguments[name]
185	                if val is not None:
186	                    _mark_dynamic_on_value(val, dims)
187	
188	        # Avoid cross-instance cache reuse
189	        torch._dynamo.eval_frame.remove_from_cache(unbound_fwd.__code__)
190	
191	        bound = types.MethodType(unbound_fwd, self)
192	        compiled_callable = torch.compile(
193	            bound, fullgraph=fullgraph, backend=backend_factory
194	        )
195	
196	        # Trigger Dynamo so bytecode hook can capture
197	        compiled_callable(*args, **kwargs)
198	
199	        state["compiled"] = True
200	        state["compiled_callable"] = compiled_callable
201	
202	    def trampoline(self, *args, **kwargs):
203	        use_compiled = _COMPILE_ENABLED.get()
204	        if use_compiled:
205	            if not state["compiled"]:
206	                _ensure_compiled(self, *args, **kwargs)
207	
208	            compiled_callable = state["compiled_callable"]
209	            return compiled_callable(*args, **kwargs)
210	        else:
211	            # Explicitly run the original uncompiled forward
212	            return unbound_fwd(self, *args, **kwargs)
213	
214	    module.forward = types.MethodType(trampoline, module)
215	    return module
216

1550	                self.kv_cache_dtype = torch.float8_e4m3fn
1551	        elif self.server_args.kv_cache_dtype in ("bf16", "bfloat16"):
1552	            self.kv_cache_dtype = torch.bfloat16
1553	        elif self.server_args.kv_cache_dtype == "fp4_e2m1":
1554	            if hasattr(torch, "float4_e2m1fn_x2"):
1555	                self.kv_cache_dtype = torch.float4_e2m1fn_x2
1556	                logger.warning(f"FP4 (E2M1) KV Cache might lead to a accuracy drop!")
1557	            else:
1558	                logger.warning(
1559	                    f"--kv-cache-dtype falls back to 'auto' because this torch version does not support torch.float4_e2m1fn_x2"
1560	                )
1561	                self.kv_cache_dtype = self.dtype
1562	        else:
1563	            raise ValueError(
1564	                f"Unsupported kv_cache_dtype: {self.server_args.kv_cache_dtype}."
1565	            )
1566	
1567	        log_info_on_rank0(logger, f"Using KV cache dtype: {self.kv_cache_dtype}")
1568	
1569	    def init_cublas(self):
1570	        """We need to run a small matmul to init cublas. Otherwise, it will raise some errors later."""
1571	        dtype = torch.float16
1572	        device = "cuda"
1573	        a = torch.ones((16, 16), dtype=dtype, device=device)
1574	        b = torch.ones((16, 16), dtype=dtype, device=device)
1575	        c = a @ b
1576	        return c
1577	
1578	    def init_attention_backend(self):
1579	        """Init attention kernel backend."""

31	class AttentionType(Enum):
32	    """
33	    Attention type.
34	    Use string to be compatible with `torch.compile`.
35	    """
36	
37	    # Decoder attention between previous layer Q/K/V
38	    DECODER = "decoder"
39	    # Encoder attention between previous layer Q/K/V
40	    ENCODER_ONLY = "encoder_only"
41	
42	
43	class RadixAttention(nn.Module):
44	    """
45	    The attention layer implementation.
46	    """
47	
48	    def __init__(
49	        self,
50	        num_heads: int,
51	        head_dim: int,
52	        scaling: float,
53	        num_kv_heads: int,
54	        layer_id: int,
55	        logit_cap: float = 0.0,
56	        v_head_dim: int = -1,
57	        sliding_window_size: int = -1,
58	        is_cross_attention: bool = False,
59	        pos_encoding_mode: str = "NONE",
60	        logit_capping_method: str = "tanh",
61	        quant_config: Optional[QuantizationConfig] = None,
62	        attn_type: AttentionType = AttentionType.DECODER,
63	        use_irope: bool = False,
64	        prefix: str = "",
65	    ):
66	        super().__init__()
67	        self.tp_q_head_num = num_heads
68	        self.tp_k_head_num = num_kv_heads
69	        self.tp_v_head_num = num_kv_heads
70	        self.head_dim = head_dim
71	        self.qk_head_dim = head_dim
72	        self.v_head_dim = v_head_dim if v_head_dim != -1 else head_dim
73	        self.scaling = scaling
74	        self.layer_id = layer_id
75	        self.logit_cap = logit_cap
76	        self.sliding_window_size = sliding_window_size or -1
77	        self.is_cross_attention = is_cross_attention
78	        self.use_irope = use_irope
79	        self.k_scale = None
80	        self.v_scale = None
81	        self.k_scale_float = None
82	        self.v_scale_float = None
83	        self.quant_method = None
84	
85	        if quant_config is not None:
86	            self.quant_method = quant_config.get_quant_method(self, prefix=prefix)
87	        if self.quant_method is not None:
88	            self.quant_method.create_weights(self)
89	        self.attn_type = attn_type
90	
91	        self.pos_encoding_mode = pos_encoding_mode
92	        self.logit_capping_method = logit_capping_method
93	        self.xai_temperature_len = -1
94	
95	    def forward(
96	        self,
97	        q,
98	        k,
99	        v,
100	        forward_batch: ForwardBatch,
101	        save_kv_cache: bool = True,
102	        **kwargs,
103	    ):
104	        if k is not None:
105	            # For cross-layer sharing, kv can be None
106	            assert v is not None
107	            if "k_rope" not in kwargs:
108	                k = k.view(-1, self.tp_k_head_num, self.qk_head_dim)
109	                v = v.view(-1, self.tp_v_head_num, self.v_head_dim)
110	            else:
111	                k = k.view(-1, self.tp_k_head_num, self.v_head_dim)
112	
113	        # Phase 3: Auto-pass sparse_metadata from forward_batch to backend if available
114	        # This allows backends like MiniCPMSparseBackend to use frontend-computed metadata
115	        if hasattr(forward_batch, "sparse_metadata") and forward_batch.sparse_metadata is not None:
116	            kwargs["sparse_metadata"] = forward_batch.sparse_metadata
117	
118	        if forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend() and get_forward_context() is not None:
119	            if self.qk_head_dim != self.v_head_dim:
120	                output = q.new_empty((q.shape[0], self.tp_q_head_num * self.v_head_dim))
121	            else:
122	                output = torch.empty_like(q)
123	            unified_attention_with_output(
124	                q, k, v, output, save_kv_cache, self.layer_id, **kwargs
125	            )
126	            return output
127	        else:
128	            return forward_batch.attn_backend.forward(
129	                q,
130	                k,
131	                v,
132	                self,
133	                forward_batch,
134	                save_kv_cache,
135	                **kwargs,
136	            )
137	
138	
139	@register_custom_op(mutates_args=["output"])
140	def unified_attention_with_output(
141	    query: torch.Tensor,
142	    key: torch.Tensor,
143	    value: torch.Tensor,
144	    output: torch.Tensor,
145	    save_kv_cache: bool,
146	    layer_id: int,
147	    *,
148	    q_rope: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
149	    k_rope: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
150	    sinks: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
151	) -> None:
152	    context = get_forward_context()
153	    forward_batch = context.forward_batch
154	    attention_layers = context.attention_layers
155	    attention_layer = attention_layers[layer_id]
156	
157	    kwargs = {}
158	    if q_rope is not None:
159	        kwargs["q_rope"] = q_rope
160	    if k_rope is not None:
161	        kwargs["k_rope"] = k_rope
162	    if sinks is not None:
163	        kwargs["sinks"] = sinks
164	
165	    ret = forward_batch.attn_backend.forward(
166	        query, key, value, attention_layer, forward_batch, save_kv_cache, **kwargs
167	    )
168	    assert (
169	        output.numel() == ret.numel()
170	    ), f"Output tensor element mismatch: {output.numel()} != {ret.numel()}"
171	
172	    output.view(ret.shape).copy_(ret)
173	    return
174

8	F = TypeVar("F", bound=Callable)

10	
11	@overload
12	def register_custom_op(
13	    fn: F,
14	    *,
15	    op_name: Optional[str] = None,
16	    mutates_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
17	    out_shape: Optional[Union[int, str]] = None,
18	    eager: bool = True,

42	
43	@overload
44	def register_custom_op(
45	    *,
46	    op_name: Optional[str] = None,
47	    mutates_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
48	    fake_impl: Optional[Callable],
49	    eager: bool = True,
50	) -> Callable[[F], F]: ...
51	
52	
53	# Real implementation
54	def register_custom_op(
55	    fn: Optional[Callable] = None,
56	    *,
57	    op_name: Optional[str] = None,
58	    mutates_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
59	    eager: bool = True,
60	    **extra_kwargs,
61	) -> Any:
62	    """
63	    A decorator to register a custom operator.
64	
65	    Example usage:
66	    ```python
67	    # inplace operator, out_shape is None by default
68	    @register_custom_op(mutates_args=["x"])
69	    def add_1_(x: torch.Tensor) -> None:
70	        x.add_(1)
71	
72	    # operator with output, out_shape indicates the position of output
73	    @register_custom_op(mutates_args=["x"], out_shape=0)
74	    def add(x: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
75	        return x.add_(y)
76	    ```
77	
78	    :param fn: The function to be registered as a custom operator.
79	               If None, return a decorator.
80	    :type fn: Callable
81	    :param op_name: The name of the operator. If None, use the function name
82	    :type op_name: Optional[str]
83	    :param mutates_args: A list of argument names that are mutated in-place.
84	    :type mutates_args: List[str]
85	    :param out_shape: The position (int for positional, str for keyword) of the output-shape tensor.
86	                      It is used to generate a fake implementation for torch.compile compatibility.
87	                      If the operator is inplace and has no output, set to None.
88	    :type out_shape: Optional[List[Union[int, str]]]
89	    :param fake_impl: A fake implementation for the operator.
90	                      Only one of `out_shape` or `fake_impl` should be provided.
91	    :type fake_impl: Optional[Callable]
92	    :param eager: Whether to register the operator eagerly.
93	                  If False, the registration will be deferred until the first call.
94	                  If you met any issue with torch.compile, try to set eager=True.
95	                  Currently, to avoid misuse, we set eager=True by default.
96	    :type eager: bool
97	    :return: The registered JIT custom operator, or a decorator.
98	             NOTE: the real register will occur at the first call of the function.
99	    :rtype: Callable
100	    """
101	    extra_kwarg_keys = set(extra_kwargs.keys())
102	    expected_kwarg_keys = set({"out_shape", "fake_impl"})
103	    assert (
104	        expected_kwarg_keys >= extra_kwarg_keys
105	    ), f"Unexpected extra kwargs: {extra_kwarg_keys - expected_kwarg_keys}"
106	
107	    has_out_shape = "out_shape" in extra_kwargs
108	    has_fake_impl = "fake_impl" in extra_kwargs
109	    assert not (
110	        has_out_shape and has_fake_impl
111	    ), "Only one of `out_shape` or `fake_impl` should be provided."
112	    # Assume inplace if neither out_shape nor fake_impl is provided
113	    if not (has_out_shape or has_fake_impl):
114	        extra_kwargs["out_shape"] = None
115	
116	    def decorator(op_func: Callable) -> Callable:
117	        wrapper = CustomOpWrapper(
118	            op_name=op_name or op_func.__name__,
119	            op_func=op_func,
120	            mutates_args=mutates_args or [],
121	            **extra_kwargs,
122	        )
123	        return wrapper.real_impl if eager else wrapper
124	
125	    if fn is not None:
126	        return decorator(fn)
127	    return decorator
128	
129	
130	class CustomOpWrapper:
131	    def __init__(
132	        self,
133	        op_name: str,
134	        op_func: Callable,
135	        mutates_args: List[str],
136	        **extra_kwargs,
137	    ):
138	        self.op_name = op_name
139	        self.op_func = op_func
140	        self.mutates_args = mutates_args
141	        self.extra_kwargs = extra_kwargs
142	        self._impl: Optional[Callable] = None
143	
144	    def __call__(self, *args, **kwargs):
145	        return self.real_impl(*args, **kwargs)
146	
147	    @property
148	    def real_impl(self) -> Callable:
149	        if self._impl is None:
150	            if not hasattr(torch.ops.sglang, self.op_name):
151	                from sglang.srt.utils.common import direct_register_custom_op
152	
153	                # NOTE(dark): if torch compile fail here, mark the decorator as eager
154	                # lazy registration does not work with torch compile
155	                direct_register_custom_op(
156	                    op_name=self.op_name,
157	                    op_func=self.op_func,
158	                    mutates_args=self.mutates_args,
159	                    fake_impl=self.fake_impl,
160	                )
161	            self._impl = getattr(torch.ops.sglang, self.op_name)
162	            assert self._impl is not None
163	        return self._impl
164	
165	    @property
166	    def fake_impl(self) -> Callable:
167	        if "fake_impl" in self.extra_kwargs:
168	            return self.extra_kwargs["fake_impl"]
169	        assert "out_shape" in self.extra_kwargs
170	        signature = inspect.signature(self.op_func)
171	        out_shape = self.extra_kwargs["out_shape"]
172	        # check out_shape in signature
173	
174	        def fake_impl(*args, **kwargs):
175	            if out_shape is None:
176	                return None
177	            bound = signature.bind(*args, **kwargs)
178	            bound.apply_defaults()
179	            try:
180	                return torch.empty_like(
181	                    bound.args[out_shape]
182	                    if isinstance(out_shape, int)
183	                    else bound.arguments[out_shape]
184	                )
185	            except (IndexError, KeyError):
186	                raise RuntimeError(
187	                    f"Cannot find output argument at position `{out_shape}` for "
188	                    f"custom operator `{self.op_name}` with signature `{signature}`."
189	                )
190	
191	        return fake_impl
192

253	_MM_EVT_LOG: list = []   # list of (start_event, end_event, bucket)
254	_MM_EVT_TOTALS: dict = {}  # bucket -> accumulated ms

257	
258	def _evt_record(bucket: str):
259	    """Return (start_evt, end_evt) and enqueue into log. Call .record() on start."""
260	    if not _MM_PROFILE:
261	        return None, None
262	    try:
263	        if torch.cuda.is_current_stream_capturing():
264	            return None, None
265	    except RuntimeError:
266	        return None, None
267	    s = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
268	    e = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
269	    s.record()
270	    _MM_EVT_LOG.append((s, e, bucket))
271	    return s, e
272	
273	
274	def _evt_end(end_evt):
275	    """Record the end event."""
276	    if end_evt is not None:
277	        end_evt.record()
278	
279	
280	def _evt_flush():
281	    """Synchronize and accumulate all pending events. Returns dict of bucket→ms for this flush."""
282	    global _MM_EVT_LOG
283	    if not _MM_EVT_LOG:
284	        return {}
285	    torch.cuda.synchronize()
286	    delta: dict = {}
287	    for s, e, bucket in _MM_EVT_LOG:
288	        try:
289	            ms = s.elapsed_time(e)
290	        except Exception:
291	            ms = 0.0
292	        delta[bucket] = delta.get(bucket, 0.0) + ms
293	        _MM_EVT_TOTALS[bucket] = _MM_EVT_TOTALS.get(bucket, 0.0) + ms
294	        _MM_EVT_COUNTS[bucket] = _MM_EVT_COUNTS.get(bucket, 0) + 1
295	    _MM_EVT_LOG = []
296	    return delta

1149	        q = q.reshape(-1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
1150	        k = k.reshape(-1, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
1151	        v = v.reshape(-1, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
1152	
1153	        q = q.unsqueeze(0)
1154	        k = k.unsqueeze(0)
1155	        v = v.unsqueeze(0)
1156	
1157	        attn_backend = forward_batch.attn_backend
1158	        if not hasattr(attn_backend, "linear_attn_backend"):
1159	            raise RuntimeError(
1160	                "SimpleGLAAttnBackend requires HybridLinearAttnBackend but got "
1161	                f"{type(attn_backend).__name__}. This mixer should only be used for "
1162	                "MiniCPM hybrid models."
1163	            )
1164	
1165	        linear_attn_backend = attn_backend.linear_attn_backend
1166	        if not isinstance(linear_attn_backend, SimpleGLAAttnBackend):
1167	            raise RuntimeError(
1168	                f"Expected SimpleGLAAttnBackend but got {type(linear_attn_backend).__name__}"
1169	            )
1170	
1171	        s, e = _evt_record("gla_kernel_ms")
1172	        o = linear_attn_backend.forward(
1173	            q=q, k=k, v=v,
1174	            forward_batch=forward_batch,
1175	            layer_id=self.layer_id,
1176	            output_attentions=False,
1177	        )
1178	        _evt_end(e)
1179	
1180	        # Fused RMSNorm + sigmoid_mul fast path: when both output_norm and
1181	        # output_gate are on, fold (o_norm + z_proj + sigmoid_mul) into:
1182	        #   z = z_proj(hidden_states)
1183	        #   o = fused_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul(o, z, o_norm_w)
1184	        # z_proj is independent of o so reordering is safe.
1185	        if (
1186	            _MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL
1187	            and _HAS_TRITON
1188	            and self.use_output_norm
1189	            and self.use_output_gate
1190	            and o.is_cuda and o.is_contiguous()
1191	        ):
1192	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_z_proj_ms"); z, _ = self.z_proj(hidden_states); _evt_end(e)
1193	            s, e = _evt_record("gla_o_norm_sigmoid_mul_ms")
1194	            o = _minicpm_gla_rmsnorm_sigmoid_mul(o, z, self.o_norm.weight, float(self.rms_norm_eps))
1195	            _evt_end(e)
1196	        else:
1197	            if self.use_output_norm:
1198	                s, e = _evt_record("gla_o_norm_ms"); o = self.o_norm(o); _evt_end(e)
1199	
1200	            if self.use_output_gate:
1201	                s, e = _evt_record("gla_z_proj_ms"); z, _ = self.z_proj(hidden_states); _evt_end(e)
1202	                s, e = _evt_record("gla_sigmoid_mul_ms")
1203	                if _MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_SIGMOID_MUL and _HAS_TRITON and o.is_cuda and o.is_contiguous() and z.is_contiguous() and o.shape == z.shape:
1204	                    o = _minicpm_gla_sigmoid_mul(o, z)
1205	                else:
1206	                    o = o * F.sigmoid(z)
1207	                _evt_end(e)
1208	
1209	        s, e = _evt_record("gla_o_proj_ms"); y, _ = self.o_proj(o); _evt_end(e)
1210	        return y
1211	
1212	
1213	class MiniCPMDecoderLayer(nn.Module):

1215	        self,
1216	        config,
1217	        layer_id: int = 0,
1218	        quant_config: Optional[QuantizationConfig] = None,
1219	        prefix: str = "",

2248	    def init_forward_metadata_capture_cuda_graph(
2249	        self,
2250	        bs: int,
2251	        num_tokens: int,
2252	        req_pool_indices: torch.Tensor,
2253	        seq_lens: torch.Tensor,
2254	        encoder_lens: Optional[torch.Tensor],
2255	        forward_mode: ForwardMode,
2256	        spec_info: Optional[SpecInput],
2257	    ):
2258	        """Initialize forward metadata for capturing CUDA graph.
2259	
2260	        For FlashInfer sparse mode (tokens > 8192), uses wrapper state persistence pattern:
2261	
2262	        Key Challenge:
2263	        - sparse_page_table is computed DYNAMICALLY during forward() based on query vectors
2264	        - FlashInfer requires kv_indptr/kv_indices converted BEFORE CUDA graph replay
2265	        - Direct conversion fails because converted data becomes stale by replay time
2266	
2267	        Solution: Wrapper State Persistence Pattern
2268	        1. Create wrapper with BUFFER VIEWS (slices of pre-allocated tensors)
2269	        2. Store wrapper references (addresses) - NOT the tensor data
2270	        3. Before each replay: UPDATE UNDERLYING STORAGE at those addresses
2271	        4. Call fast_decode_plan() to sync wrapper's cached pointers/metadata
2272	        5. Wrapper accesses updated storage through same addresses
2273	
2274	        Example:
2275	        - Capture: wrapper = BatchDecodeWrapper(paged_kv_indptr_buffer=full[:bs+1], ...)
2276	        - Replay (before graph): full[:bs+1].copy_(new_data)  # Update storage
2277	        - Replay (before graph): fast_decode_plan(wrapper, ...)  # Sync pointers
2278	        - Graph execution: wrapper uses updated data via same addresses
2279	
2280	        For sparse mode:
2281	        - kv_indptr is PRECOMPUTED and static: [0, K, 2K, ..., bs*K]
2282	        - Only kv_indices needs updating per request
2283	        - 128x memory reduction: bs*max_num_pages -> bs*num_sparse_topk_tokens
2284	        """
2285	        metadata = MiniCPMBackendMetadata()
2286	
2287	        device = seq_lens.device
2288	        if forward_mode.is_decode_or_idle():
2289	            metadata.cache_seqlens_int32 = seq_lens.to(torch.int32)
2290	            batch_size = len(seq_lens)
2291	            device = seq_lens.device
2292	            metadata.cu_seqlens_k = torch.nn.functional.pad(
2293	                torch.cumsum(seq_lens, dim=0, dtype=torch.int32), (1, 0)
2294	            )
2295	            metadata.max_seq_len_k = seq_lens.max().item()
2296	            metadata.page_table = self.decode_cuda_graph_metadata["page_table"][:bs, :]
2297	
2298	            metadata.cu_seqlens_q = torch.arange(
2299	                0, batch_size + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
